-- [[ BROCCOLI HUB - Premium Code Sniper Auto code redeem + sound + emoji bg | UI Reference ]]
local cloneref = cloneref or function(object) return object end
local Players = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
local ReplicatedStorage = cloneref(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"))
local RunService = cloneref(game:GetService("RunService"))
local UserInputService = cloneref(game:GetService("UserInputService"))
local HttpService = cloneref(game:GetService("HttpService"))
local TweenService = cloneref(game:GetService("TweenService"))
local SoundService = cloneref(game:GetService("SoundService"))

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

if getgenv and getgenv().StopAura then
    pcall(getgenv().StopAura)
end

-- ==================== PLAY EPIC SOUND ====================
task.spawn(function()
    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://1516791621"
    sound.Volume = 2
    sound.Parent = SoundService
    sound:Play()
    sound.Ended:Connect(function()
        sound:Destroy()
    end)
end)

-- CONFIGURATION SYSTEM --
local CONFIG_FILE = "broccoli_hub_config.json"
local savedConfig = {
    codeSniper = true,
    autoSubmit = true,
    submitAfter = 1,
    submitMode = "VIP",
    retypeInvalid = true,
    riddleSolver = false,
}

pcall(function()
    if type(isfile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and isfile(CONFIG_FILE) then
        local decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(CONFIG_FILE))
        if type(decoded) == "table" then
            if type(decoded.codeSniper) == "boolean" then savedConfig.codeSniper = decoded.codeSniper end
            if type(decoded.autoSubmit) == "boolean" then savedConfig.autoSubmit = decoded.autoSubmit end
            if type(decoded.submitAfter) == "number" then savedConfig.submitAfter = math.clamp(math.floor(decoded.submitAfter), 1, 5) end
            if type(decoded.submitMode) == "string" then savedConfig.submitMode = decoded.submitMode end
            if type(decoded.retypeInvalid) == "boolean" then savedConfig.retypeInvalid = decoded.retypeInvalid end
            if type(decoded.riddleSolver) == "boolean" then savedConfig.riddleSolver = decoded.riddleSolver end
        end
    end
end)

local function saveConfig()
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
    pcall(function()
        writefile(CONFIG_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode({
            codeSniper = savedConfig.codeSniper,
            autoSubmit = savedConfig.autoSubmit,
            submitAfter = savedConfig.submitAfter,
            submitMode = savedConfig.submitMode,
            retypeInvalid = savedConfig.retypeInvalid,
            riddleSolver = savedConfig.riddleSolver,
        }))
    end)
end

local function normalizeRiddleKey(q)
    if type(q) ~= "string" then return "" end
    q = q:lower():gsub("%s+"," "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
    q = q:gsub("^what is the ",""):gsub("^what is ",""):gsub("^what are ",""):gsub("^what was ",""):gsub("^whats ",""):gsub("^what's ",""):gsub("^what the ",""):gsub("^what ","")
    q = q:gsub("^who is the ",""):gsub("^who is ",""):gsub("^who ","")
    q = q:gsub("^which is the ",""):gsub("^which is ",""):gsub("^which ","")
    q = q:gsub("^name the ",""):gsub("^tell me the ",""):gsub("^give me the ","")
    q = q:gsub("[%?%.%!]+$",""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
    q = q:gsub("^y cat name", "my cat name"):gsub("%sy cat name", " my cat name")
    q = q:gsub("^y car", "my car"):gsub("coulr","color"):gsub("realsed","released"):gsub("secert","secret")
    return q
end

RIDDLE_DB["what my cat"] = "NOVA"
RIDDLE_DB["my cat"] = "NOVA"

RIDDLE_DB["my car color"] = "RED"
RIDDLE_DB["best secret"] = "LOSDRAGONS"
RIDDLE_DB["best secret in sab"] = "LOSDRAGONS"

-- Dynamic mutation finder
local function getCurrentMutation()
    for _, gui in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
        if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Enabled then
            for _, obj in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") then
                    local txt = (obj.Text or ""):lower()
                    if txt:find("yin") and txt:find("yang") then return "YINYANG" end
                    if txt:find("mutation") then
                        if txt:find("diamond") then return "DIAMOND" end
                        if txt:find("gold") then return "GOLD" end
                        if txt:find("rainbow") then return "RAINBOW" end
                        if txt:find("galaxy") then return "GALAXY" end
                        if txt:find("lava") then return "LAVA" end
                        if txt:find("candy") then return "CANDY" end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function extractMutationFromText(t)
    if not t then return nil end
    local l = t:lower()
    if l:find("yin") and l:find("yang") then return "YINYANG" end
    local muts = {"diamond","gold","rainbow","galaxy","lava","candy","blood","neon","ice","fire","ghost"}
    for _, m in ipairs(muts) do if l:find(m) then return m:upper() end end
    return nil
end

for i=0,500 do if not RIDDLE_DB[tostring(i)] then RIDDLE_DB[tostring(i)]=tostring(i) end end

local function tokenSimilarity(a,b)
    local ta,tb={},{}; for w in a:gmatch("%w+") do ta[w]=true end; for w in b:gmatch("%w+") do tb[w]=true end
    local common,total=0,0; for w in pairs(ta) do total=total+1; if tb[w] then common=common+1 end end; for w in pairs(tb) do if not ta[w] then total=total+1 end end
    if total==0 then return 0 end; return common/total
end

local function levenshtein(s,t)
    local ls,lt=#s,#t; if ls==0 then return lt end; if lt==0 then return ls end
    local v0,v1={},{}; for i=0,lt do v0[i]=i end; for i=1,ls do v1[0]=i; local sc=s:sub(i,i); for j=1,lt do local cost=(sc==t:sub(j,j)) and 0 or 1; v1[j]=math.min(v1[j-1]+1,v0[j]+1,v0[j-1]+cost) end; for j=0,lt do v0[j]=v1[j] end end; return v0[lt]
end

local _sortedRiddleKeys={}
for k in pairs(RIDDLE_DB) do table.insert(_sortedRiddleKeys,k) end
table.sort(_sortedRiddleKeys,function(a,b) if #a~=#b then return #a>#b end local ca,cb=0,0; for _ in a:gmatch("%w+") do ca=ca+1 end; for _ in b:gmatch("%w+") do cb=cb+1 end; return ca>cb end)

local function solveMath(expr)
    if not expr then return nil end
    expr = expr:lower():gsub("%s+",""):gsub("plus","+"):gsub("minus","-"):gsub("times","*"):gsub("x","*"):gsub("dividedby","/"):gsub("divide","/"):gsub("over","/")
    if not expr:match("^[%d%+%-%*/%(%)]+$") then return nil end
    if #expr>25 then return nil end
    if not expr:match("%d") then return nil end
    local fn = loadstring("return ("..expr..")")
    if not fn then return nil end
    local ok,res=pcall(fn)
    if ok and type(res)=="number" and res>=-100000 and res<=100000 then
        if math.floor(res)==res then return tostring(math.floor(res)) else return tostring(res) end
    end
    return nil
end

-- ============================================================
-- SMART DATE / DAY / MONTH / YEAR (global, realtime)
-- ============================================================
local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_getNow()
    local t = os.date("*t")
    local dayNames = {"SUNDAY","MONDAY","TUESDAY","WEDNESDAY","THURSDAY","FRIDAY","SATURDAY"}
    local monthNames = {"JANUARY","FEBRUARY","MARCH","APRIL","MAY","JUNE","JULY","AUGUST","SEPTEMBER","OCTOBER","NOVEMBER","DECEMBER"}
    local dayName = dayNames[t.wday] or "SATURDAY"
    local monthName = monthNames[t.month] or "SEPTEMBER"
    local dayNum = tostring(t.day)
    local year = tostring(t.year)
    local monthNum = string.format("%02d", t.month)
    local dayNumPad = string.format("%02d", t.day)
    return {
        dayName = dayName,
        monthName = monthName,
        dayNum = dayNum,
        year = year,
        monthNum = monthNum,
        dayNumPad = dayNumPad,
        fullDate = monthName .. dayNum .. year,
        dateShort = monthName .. dayNum,
        iso = year .. monthNum .. dayNumPad,
        slash = monthNum .. "/" .. dayNumPad .. "/" .. year,
        dayMonth = dayName .. monthName,
        monthYear = monthName .. year,
        dayYear = dayName .. year,
    }
end

local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_solveSmartDate(question)
    if not question or question == "" then return nil end
    local q = tostring(question):lower()
    q = q:gsub("[%?%!%.%,%:%;]+", " ")
    q = q:gsub("%s+", " "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
    q = q:gsub("todays", "today"):gsub("today'?s", "today")
    q = q:gsub("wut", "what"):gsub("wat", "what"):gsub("wht", "what")
    q = q:gsub("goin+", "going"):gsub("gona", "going"):gsub("gonna", "going")
    q = q:gsub("currnt", "current"):gsub("curent", "current")
    q = q:gsub("monthe?", "month"):gsub("yeare?", "year"):gsub("daay", "day"):gsub("dte", "date")
    q = q:gsub("teh", "the")

    -- skip historical / static questions (birth, sab release, etc.)
    if q:find("birth", 1, true) or q:find("born", 1, true) or q:find("created", 1, true)
        or q:find("released", 1, true) or q:find("made", 1, true) or q:find("sab ", 1, true)
        or q:find("game", 1, true) or q:find("how old", 1, true) or q:find("sammy age", 1, true)
        or q:find("age", 1, true) and not q:find("today", 1, true) then
        return nil
    end

    local now = _BROCCOLI_HUB_getNow()

    -- DAY OF THE MONTH (number) — must be BEFORE day-of-week
    if q:find("day of the month", 1, true) or q:find("day of month", 1, true)
        or q:match("day%s+of%s+the%s+month") or q:match("day%s+of%s+month")
        or q:match("the%s+day%s+of%s+the%s+month") or q:match("what%s+day%s+of%s+the%s+month")
        or q:match("which%s+day%s+of%s+the%s+month") or q:match("todays?%s+day%s+of%s+the%s+month")
        or q:match("day%s+number") or q:match("date%s+number") or q:match("the%s+day%s+number")
        or q:match("what%s+day%s+number") or q:match("number%s+of%s+the%s+day")
        or q:match("what%s+is%s+the%s+day%s+of%s+the%s+month") then
        return now.dayNum
    end

    -- DAY OF WEEK
    if q:match("day%s+of%s+the%s+week") or q:match("day%s+of%s+week")
        or q:match("^what%s+day") or q:match("^which%s+day") or q:match("^day%s+today")
        or q:match("^today%s+day") or q:match("^current%s+day")
        or q:match("day%s+is%s+it") or q:match("day%s+going") or q:match("day%s+now")
        or q:match("what%s+day%s+is") or q:match("whats?%s+the%s+day")
        or q:match("^day$") or q:match("^the%s+day$")
        or q:match("what%s+day%s+are%s+we") or q:match("what%s+day%s+we")
        or q:match("todays?%s+day") or q:match("day%s+it%s+is")
        or q:match("what%s+day%s+going") or q:match("day%s+going")
        -- "the day" alone only if NOT "day of the month"
        or (q:match("^the%s+day") and not q:find("month", 1, true)) then
        return now.dayName
    end

    -- MONTH
    if q:match("^what%s+month") or q:match("^which%s+month") or q:match("^month%s+today")
        or q:match("^today%s+month") or q:match("^current%s+month") or q:match("^this%s+month")
        or q:match("month%s+is%s+it") or q:match("month%s+now") or q:match("month%s+going")
        or q:match("whats?%s+the%s+month") or q:match("^month$") or q:match("^the%s+month$")
        or q:match("todays?%s+month") or q:match("what%s+month%s+are%s+we") then
        return now.monthName
    end

    -- YEAR
    if q:match("^what%s+year") or q:match("^which%s+year") or q:match("^year%s+today")
        or q:match("^today%s+year") or q:match("^current%s+year") or q:match("^this%s+year")
        or q:match("year%s+is%s+it") or q:match("year%s+now") or q:match("year%s+going")
        or q:match("whats?%s+the%s+year") or q:match("^year$") or q:match("^the%s+year$")
        or q:match("todays?%s+year") or q:match("what%s+year%s+are%s+we")
        or q:match("what%s+year%s+is%s+it") then
        return now.year
    end

    -- DATE
    if q:match("^what%s+date") or q:match("^which%s+date") or q:match("^date%s+today")
        or q:match("^today%s+date") or q:match("^current%s+date") or q:match("^todays?%s+date")
        or q:match("date%s+is%s+it") or q:match("date%s+now") or q:match("whats?%s+the%s+date")
        or q:match("^date$") or q:match("^the%s+date$") or q:match("what%s+is%s+the%s+date")
        or q:match("full%s+date") or q:match("complete%s+date") then
        return now.fullDate
    end

    if q:match("day%s+and%s+month") or q:match("month%s+and%s+day") or q:match("day%s+with%s+month")
        or q:match("month%s+with%s+day") then
        return now.dayName .. now.monthName
    end
    if q:match("month%s+and%s+year") or q:match("year%s+and%s+month") or q:match("month%s+with%s+year") then
        return now.monthName .. now.year
    end
    if q:match("day%s+and%s+year") or q:match("year%s+and%s+day") or q:match("day%s+with%s+year") then
        return now.dayName .. now.year
    end
    if q:match("day%s+month%s+year") or q:match("date%s+full") or q:match("today%s+full") then
        return now.fullDate
    end
    if q:match("month%s+day") or q:match("date%s+short") or q:match("short%s+date") then
        return now.dateShort
    end

    -- loose fallback
    if q:find("day", 1, true) and (q:find("today", 1, true) or q:find("now", 1, true) or q:find("current", 1, true) or q:find("going", 1, true) or q:find("what", 1, true) or q:find("which", 1, true)) then
        if not q:find("month", 1, true) and not q:find("year", 1, true) and not q:find("date", 1, true) and not q:find("number", 1, true) then
            return now.dayName
        end
    end
    if q:find("month", 1, true) and (q:find("today", 1, true) or q:find("now", 1, true) or q:find("current", 1, true) or q:find("this", 1, true) or q:find("what", 1, true) or q:find("which", 1, true)) then
        if not q:find("year", 1, true) and not q:find("day", 1, true) then
            return now.monthName
        end
    end
    if q:find("year", 1, true) and (q:find("today", 1, true) or q:find("now", 1, true) or q:find("current", 1, true) or q:find("this", 1, true) or q:find("what", 1, true) or q:find("which", 1, true)) then
        if not q:find("month", 1, true) and not q:find("day", 1, true) and not q:find("birth", 1, true) then
            return now.year
        end
    end
    if q:find("date", 1, true) and (q:find("today", 1, true) or q:find("now", 1, true) or q:find("current", 1, true) or q:find("what", 1, true) or q:find("which", 1, true)) then
        return now.fullDate
    end
    return nil
end

if getgenv then
    getgenv()._BROCCOLI_HUB_solveSmartDate = _BROCCOLI_HUB_solveSmartDate
    getgenv()._BROCCOLI_HUB_getNow = _BROCCOLI_HUB_getNow
end

local function solveSingleEnhanced(part)
    if not part or part=="" then return nil end
    -- PURE NUMBER CHECK FIRST - if part is just numbers, return it directly
    if part:match("^%d+$") then return part end
    -- Handle numbers with spaces like 5555
    local trimmed = part:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    if trimmed and trimmed:match("^%d+$") then return trimmed end
    -- SMART DATE first
    local dateAns = _BROCCOLI_HUB_solveSmartDate(part)
    if dateAns then return dateAns end
    local lower = part:lower()
    -- dynamic mutation check
    if lower:find("mutation") then
        local dyn = getCurrentMutation()
        if dyn then return dyn end
        local ext = extractMutationFromText(part)
        if ext then return ext end
        -- if query is exactly about mutation running, try dynamic, else fallback to YINYANG
        if lower:find("run now") or lower:find("running") or lower:find("current") then
            return "YINYANG"
        end
    end
    -- math direct
    local mathAns = solveMath(part)
    if mathAns then return mathAns end
    local mathExpr = part:match("(%d+%s*[%+%-%*/]%s*%d+)")
    if mathExpr then local ma=solveMath(mathExpr); if ma then return ma end end
    
    local key=normalizeRiddleKey(part)
    if key=="" then return nil end
    if RIDDLE_DB[key] then return RIDDLE_DB[key] end
    if RIDDLE_DB[key:gsub("%s+"," ")] then return RIDDLE_DB[key:gsub("%s+"," ")] end
    
    -- longest substring match - prioritize longer keys
    for _, k in ipairs(_sortedRiddleKeys) do
        if #k>=3 and key:find(k,1,true) then
            return RIDDLE_DB[k]
        end
    end
    
    -- token similarity
    local bestSim,bestAns=0,nil
    for k,v in pairs(RIDDLE_DB) do
        local sim=tokenSimilarity(key,k)
        if sim>bestSim and sim>=0.5 then bestSim=sim; bestAns=v end
    end
    if bestAns then return bestAns end
    
    for k,v in pairs(RIDDLE_DB) do
        if #k>=4 then
            local lev=levenshtein(key,k)
            if lev<=2 then return v end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function extractNumbers(str) local nums={}; for n in str:gmatch("%d+") do table.insert(nums,n) end; return nums end

local function findClosestAnswers(question, limit)
    limit=limit or 5
    local q=normalizeRiddleKey(question); if q=="" then return {} end
    local qNums=extractNumbers(q)
    local qNoNums=q:gsub("%d+"," "):gsub("%s+"," "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
    local scored={}
    for k,v in pairs(RIDDLE_DB) do
        local sim=tokenSimilarity(qNoNums~="" and qNoNums or q, k)
        local lev=levenshtein(q,k); local lenMax=math.max(#q,#k); local levScore=1-(lev/(lenMax+1))
        local combined=(sim*0.7)+(levScore*0.3); if q:find(k,1,true) or k:find(q,1,true) then combined=combined+0.4 end
        if #k>8 then combined=combined+0.05 end
        table.insert(scored,{key=k, ans=v, score=combined})
    end
    table.sort(scored,function(a,b) if math.abs(a.score-b.score)<0.01 then return #a.key>#b.key end return a.score>b.score end)
    local results, seenAns={}, {}
    for i=1, math.min(#scored, limit*3) do
        local ans=scored[i].ans
        if not seenAns[ans] and scored[i].score>0.15 then
            seenAns[ans]=true
            local finalAns=ans
            if #qNums>0 and not finalAns:match("%d+") then finalAns=finalAns..table.concat(qNums,"") end
            table.insert(results, finalAns)
            if #results>=limit then break end
        end
    end
    return results
end

local function analyzeFullSentence(question)
    local q = question:lower()
    local found, foundKeys = {}, {}
    for n in q:gmatch("%d+") do table.insert(found, n) end
    local lowerQ = normalizeRiddleKey(q)
    -- mutation dynamic
    local dyn = getCurrentMutation()
    if dyn and lowerQ:find("mutation") then table.insert(found, dyn); table.insert(foundKeys, "mutation") end
    for _, k in ipairs(_sortedRiddleKeys) do
        if #k>=3 and lowerQ:find(k,1,true) then
            local overlap=false
            for _, fk in ipairs(foundKeys) do if fk:find(k,1,true) or k:find(fk,1,true) then if #k<#fk then overlap=true; break end end end
            if not overlap then table.insert(found, RIDDLE_DB[k]); table.insert(foundKeys, k); if #found>=6 then break end end
        end
    end
    if #found==0 then
        local guesses=findClosestAnswers(question,3)
        for _, g in ipairs(guesses) do local ex=false; for _, f in ipairs(found) do if f==g then ex=true; break end end; if not ex then table.insert(found,g) end end
    end
    return found
end

local function solveRiddle(question)
    if not question or question=="" then return nil end
    local original=question
    local q=question:lower():gsub("[%?%!%.%:%;]+"," "):gsub("%s+"," "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
    if q=="" then return nil end
    
    -- First try to solve math in full string if it contains + - * /
    -- For "my cat name and 67+67" we need to handle AND
    -- Handle AND with math: split by AND, but keep math expressions intact
    if q:find(" and ") or q:find(" & ") then
        -- Use placeholder for math plus to avoid splitting on plus
        local placeholder = q
        -- Extract math expressions and replace with placeholders
        local mathParts = {}
        local idx=0
        placeholder = placeholder:gsub("(%d+%s*[%+%-%*/]%s*%d+)", function(m)
            idx=idx+1
            mathParts[idx]=m
            return " __MATH"..idx.."__ "
        end)
        -- Now split by and
        local tmp = placeholder:gsub("%s+and%s+", ","):gsub("%s+&%s+", ",")
        local parts={}
        for p in tmp:gmatch("[^,]+") do p=p:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$"); if p~="" then table.insert(parts,p) end end
        local results={}
        for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
            -- Restore math placeholders
            p = p:gsub("__MATH(%d+)__", function(n) return mathParts[tonumber(n)] or "" end)
            p = p:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if p:match("^%d+$") then table.insert(results, p)
            else
                local ma = solveMath(p)
                if ma then table.insert(results, ma)
                else
                    -- check if p contains math inside like "67+67"
                    local innerMath = p:match("(%d+%s*[%+%-%*/]%s*%d+)")
                    if innerMath then
                        local innerAns = solveMath(innerMath)
                        if innerAns then
                            -- remove math part and solve remaining + math result
                            local remaining = p:gsub(innerMath:gsub("([%+%-%*/])","%%%1"), ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                            if remaining and remaining~="" then
                                local remAns = solveSingleEnhanced(remaining)
                                if remAns then table.insert(results, remAns) end
                            end
                            table.insert(results, innerAns)
                        else
                            local ans = solveSingleEnhanced(p)
                            if ans then table.insert(results, ans) end
                        end
                    else
                        local ans = solveSingleEnhanced(p)
                        if ans then table.insert(results, ans)
                        else for n in p:gmatch("%d+") do table.insert(results, n) end end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if #results>0 then
            local deduped, seen = {}, {}
            for _, r in ipairs(results) do if not seen[r] then seen[r]=true; table.insert(deduped, r) end end
            return table.concat(deduped, "")
        end
    end
    
    -- Single part logic with TIMES handling
    local kp, rc = q:match("^(.-)%s+(%d+)%s*times?$")
    if not kp then kp, rc = q:match("^(.-)%s*x%s*(%d+)$") end
    if not kp then rc, kp = q:match("^(%d+)%s*times?%s+(.-)$") end
    if not kp then rc, kp = q:match("^(%d+)x%s+(.-)$") end
    if kp and rc then
        kp=kp:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local ans = solveSingleEnhanced(kp)
        if not ans then ans = solveMath(kp) end
        if ans then
            local r = math.min(tonumber(rc) or 1, 10)
            local rep=""; for i=1,r do rep=rep..ans end
            return rep
        end
    end
    
    -- Try direct
    local direct = solveSingleEnhanced(q)
    if direct then return direct end
    
    -- Try math in full string (like 67+67)
    local fullMath = q:match("(%d+%s*[%+%-%*/]%s*%d+)")
    if fullMath then
        local ma = solveMath(fullMath)
        if ma then
            -- Check if there's other text besides math
            local remaining = q:gsub(fullMath:gsub("([%+%-%*/])","%%%1"), ""):gsub("%s+and%s+"," "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if remaining and #remaining>2 then
                local remAns = solveSingleEnhanced(remaining)
                if remAns then return remAns .. ma end
            end
            return ma
        end
    end
    
    -- Last resort full analysis
    local fullAnalysis = analyzeFullSentence(original)
    if #fullAnalysis>0 then return table.concat(fullAnalysis, "") end
    
    return solveSingleEnhanced(original)
end



-- NAME PROTECTION: Only answer SAMMY if query explicitly starts with "my name" or is exactly about my name
local function isMyNameQuery(q)
    if not q then return false end
    local lower = q:lower():gsub("%s+"," "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
    -- Must start with my name or be exactly my name variants
    if lower:match("^my name") or lower:match("^what.*my name") or lower:match("^whats.*my name") or lower:match("^tell.*my name") or lower:match("^yo.*my name") then
        -- But not if it contains cat name, car name, dog name etc
        if lower:find("cat name") or lower:find("car") or lower:find("dog") or lower:find("beast") or lower:find("best secret") then
            return false
        end
        return true
    end
    if lower == "my name" or lower == "myname" or lower == "what my name" or lower == "whats my name" then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

-- Override solveSingleEnhanced to add name protection
local _oldSolveSingleEnhanced = solveSingleEnhanced
solveSingleEnhanced = function(part)
    local ans = _oldSolveSingleEnhanced(part)
    if ans == "SAMMY" then
        -- If answer is SAMMY but query is not explicitly about my name, check
        local lower = part:lower()
        if lower:find("cat name") or lower:find("car") or lower:find("dog name") or lower:find("beast") or lower:find("secret") or lower:find("give") or lower:find("income") then
            -- This is not a my name query, don't return SAMMY unless it's really my name query
            if not isMyNameQuery(part) then
                -- Try to find other answer for this part
                -- For example "my cat name and my name" should return both, but "my cat name" alone should not return SAMMY
                if lower:find("my cat name") then return "NOVA" end
                if lower:find("cat name") then return "NOVA" end
                -- If it's combined like "my cat name and my name", let the outer function handle it via concatenation
                return nil
            end
        end
    end
    return ans
end


-- FIX: hes name after beast/best secret = LOSDRAGONS not SAMMY
local _oldAnalyzeConcat = analyzeConcatenated
analyzeConcatenated = function(q)
    local lower = q:lower()
    -- If query asks about best/beast secret and hes name / what is it, return LOSDRAGONS
    if (lower:find("best secret") or lower:find("beast secret") or lower:find("strongest secret") or lower:find("اقوى سيكرت")) and (lower:find("hes name") or lower:find("his name") or lower:find("what is it") or lower:find("its name") or lower:find("what is") or lower:find("اسمه")) then
        -- Check if it also asks how much
        if lower:find("how much") or lower:find("كم") or lower:find("give") or lower:find("income") or lower:find("يدي") or lower:find("يعطي") then
            return {"425M", "LOSDRAGONS"}
        else
            return {"LOSDRAGONS"}
        end
    end
    return _oldAnalyzeConcat(q)
end

-- Also fix solveRiddle for this specific pattern
local _oldSolveRiddle2 = solveRiddle
solveRiddle = function(question)
    local lower = question:lower()
    if (lower:find("best secret") or lower:find("beast secret")) and lower:find("and") then
        if lower:find("hes name") or lower:find("his name") or lower:find("what is it") or lower:find("what is") then
            if lower:find("how much") then
                -- Return income + name
                return "425MLOSDRAGONS"
            else
                return "LOSDRAGONS"
            end
        end
    end
    return _oldSolveRiddle2(question)
end


-- EXIT COUNT AUTO READER - reads exit count from game UI
local function getExitCountFromGame(brainrotName)
    -- Try to find exit count tooltip in Index or Trade Plaza
    for _, gui in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
        if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
            for _, obj in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    local txt = obj.Text or ""
                    if txt:lower():find("exist") then
                        local num = txt:match("([%d,]+)")
                        if num then return num:gsub(",","") end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function getExitCountForBrainrot(name)
    if not name then return nil end
    local lower = name:lower():gsub("%s+"," ")
    -- Check RIDDLE_DB for exit count
    local key = "exit count of " .. lower
    if RIDDLE_DB[key] then return RIDDLE_DB[key] end
    key = lower .. " exit count"
    if RIDDLE_DB[key] then return RIDDLE_DB[key] end
    -- Direct name lookup in exit counts table
    for k,v in pairs(RIDDLE_DB) do
        if k:find(lower,1,true) and (k:find("exit count") or k:find("exist")) then
            return v
        end
    end
    return nil
end

-- Override solveRiddle to handle exit count queries
local _oldSolveRiddleExit = solveRiddle
solveRiddle = function(question)
    if not question then return nil end
    local lower = question:lower()
    if lower:find("exit") or lower:find("exist") or lower:find("اكزت") or lower:find("اكسزت") or lower:find("عدد") then
        -- Try to extract brainrot name from question
        local qNoSpace = lower:gsub("%s+","")
        for _, k in ipairs(_sortedRiddleKeys) do
            local kNoSpace = k:gsub("%s+",""):lower()
            if #kNoSpace >= 4 and qNoSpace:find(kNoSpace,1,true) then
                -- Found brainrot name, now get its exit count
                local brainrotName = k
                local exitKey = "exit count of " .. brainrotName:lower()
                if RIDDLE_DB[exitKey] then return RIDDLE_DB[exitKey] end
                exitKey = brainrotName:lower() .. " exit count"
                if RIDDLE_DB[exitKey] then return RIDDLE_DB[exitKey] end
                -- Try direct
                if RIDDLE_DB[brainrotName:lower()] and RIDDLE_DB[brainrotName:lower()]:match("%d+") then
                    -- This is income, not exit, skip
                end
                -- Search for exit count entry containing this brainrot name
                for dbk, dbv in pairs(RIDDLE_DB) do
                    if dbk:find(brainrotName:lower(),1,true) and (dbk:find("exit") or dbk:find("exist")) then
                        return dbv
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        -- If no specific brainrot found but question is "exit count of this"
        local dynExit = getExitCountFromGame()
        if dynExit then return dynExit end
    end
    return _oldSolveRiddleExit(question)
end


-- ============================================
-- DATABASE WITH FALLBACK SYSTEM - V19 ADVANCED
-- ============================================
local DatabaseWithFallback = {}

-- Primary DB is RIDDLE_DB itself
DatabaseWithFallback.primary = RIDDLE_DB

-- Fallback 1: Typo correction DB (common mistakes)
DatabaseWithFallback.typoDB = {
    ["mycatname"] = "NOVA",
    ["myctname"] = "NOVA",
    ["mycaatname"] = "NOVA",
    ["mycatnmae"] = "NOVA",
    ["mycatnaem"] = "NOVA",
    ["ycatname"] = "NOVA",
    ["mykatname"] = "NOVA",
    ["myname"] = "SAMMY",
    ["mynmae"] = "SAMMY",
    ["myanme"] = "SAMMY",
    ["bestsecert"] = "425M",
    ["beastsecert"] = "425M",
    ["bsetsecret"] = "425M",
    ["bestsceret"] = "425M",
}

-- Fallback 2: Live game reader (reads from UI)
function DatabaseWithFallback:readFromGame(brainrotName)
    -- Try to read exit count or income from game UI
    for _, gui in ipairs(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):GetChildren()) do
        if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
            for _, obj in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("TextLabel") and obj.Text then
                    local txt = obj.Text:lower()
                    if brainrotName and txt:find(brainrotName:lower(),1,true) then
                        -- Found brainrot tooltip, try extract numbers
                        local income = obj.Text:match("(%d+%.?%d*[KMB]/s)")
                        if income then return income:gsub("/s","") end
                        local exist = obj.Text:match("exist[s]*:%s*([%d,]+)")
                        if exist then return exist:gsub(",","") end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

-- Fallback 3: Fuzzy matcher (Levenshtein-like)
function DatabaseWithFallback:fuzzyFind(query)
    local lowerQ = query:lower():gsub("%s+","")
    local bestMatch = nil
    local bestScore = 0
    for k, v in pairs(self.primary) do
        local kNoSpace = k:lower():gsub("%s+","")
        -- Simple similarity: how many chars match
        local matchLen = 0
        for i=1, math.min(#lowerQ, #kNoSpace) do
            if lowerQ:sub(i,i) == kNoSpace:sub(i,i) then matchLen = matchLen + 1 end
        end
        local score = matchLen / math.max(#lowerQ, #kNoSpace)
        -- Also check if query contains key or key contains query
        if lowerQ:find(kNoSpace,1,true) or kNoSpace:find(lowerQ,1,true) then
            score = score + 0.5
        end
        if score > bestScore and score > 0.6 then
            bestScore = score
            bestMatch = v
        end
    end
    return bestMatch, bestScore
end

-- Fallback 4: Auto-learn (saves new riddles)
DatabaseWithFallback.learned = {}
function DatabaseWithFallback:learn(question, answer)
    self.learned[question:lower()] = answer
    RIDDLE_DB[question:lower()] = answer
    print("[DB LEARNED] " .. question .. " = " .. answer)
end

-- Main solver with fallback chain
function DatabaseWithFallback:solve(question)
    if not question or question == "" then return nil end
    local lower = question:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    
    -- Level 1: Primary DB exact match
    if self.primary[lower] then
        return self.primary[lower], "primary"
    end
    
    -- Level 2: Typo DB
    local noSpace = lower:gsub("%s+","")
    if self.typoDB[noSpace] then
        return self.typoDB[noSpace], "typo_fallback"
    end
    
    -- Level 3: Direct primary with no-space matching
    for k, v in pairs(self.primary) do
        if k:gsub("%s+","") == noSpace then
            return v, "primary_nospace"
        end
    end
    
    -- Level 4: Contains matching (for concatenated like mycatnameand5555)
    local results = analyzeConcatenated(lower)
    if results and #results > 0 then
        return table.concat(results, ""), "concat_fallback"
    end
    
    -- Level 5: Fuzzy matching
    local fuzzyAns, score = self:fuzzyFind(lower)
    if fuzzyAns and score > 0.7 then
        return fuzzyAns, "fuzzy_fallback_"..math.floor(score*100)
    end
    
    -- Level 6: Live game reading (for exit counts)
    if lower:find("exit") or lower:find("exist") or lower:find("اكزت") then
        local live = self:readFromGame(lower)
        if live then return live, "live_game_fallback" end
    end
    
    -- Level 7: Special cases for best secret
    if lower:find("best secret") or lower:find("beast secret") then
        if lower:find("name") or lower:find("what is") then
            return "LOSDRAGONS", "special_bestsecret_name"
        else
            return "425M", "special_bestsecret_income"
        end
    end
    
    return nil, "not_found"
end

-- Replace global solveRiddle to use fallback system
local _oldSolveRiddleFinal = solveRiddle
solveRiddle = function(question)
    local ans, source = DatabaseWithFallback:solve(question)
    if ans then
        print("[SOLVED via " .. source .. "] " .. question .. " -> " .. ans)
        return ans
    end
    return _oldSolveRiddleFinal(question)
end

-- Auto logger
function DatabaseWithFallback:getStats()
    local primaryCount = 0
    for _ in pairs(self.primary) do primaryCount = primaryCount + 1 end
    local learnedCount = 0
    for _ in pairs(self.learned) do learnedCount = learnedCount + 1 end
    return {
        primary = primaryCount,
        typo = #self.typoDB,
        learned = learnedCount,
        total = primaryCount + learnedCount
    }
end
-- ============================================
-- END DATABASE WITH FALLBACK
-- ============================================




-- ============================================
-- FIX: 67+67 and my name with 67 -> should give 134SAMMY67
-- لو لقى رقم بدون + او - يحطه خلاص
-- ============================================
local function solveMathSimple(expr)
    expr = expr:gsub("%s+","")
    if not expr:match("^%d+[%+%-%*/]%d+$") then return nil end
    local a, op, b = expr:match("(%d+)([%+%-%*/])(%d+)")
    a = tonumber(a); b = tonumber(b)
    if not a or not b then return nil end
    if op == "+" then return tostring(a+b)
    elseif op == "-" then return tostring(a-b)
    elseif op == "*" then return tostring(a*b)
    elseif op == "/" then return tostring(math.floor(a/b))
    end
    return nil
end

local function extractTokensInOrder(q)
    local lower = q:lower()
    local tokens = {} -- {pos, type, value, raw}
    
    -- Find math expressions first
    for start, expr, finish in lower:gmatch("()(%d+%s*[%+%-*/]%s*%d+)()") do
        table.insert(tokens, {pos=start, type="math", raw=expr, value=solveMathSimple(expr)})
    end
    
    -- Find known phrases
    local knownPhrases = {
        {"my cat name", "NOVA"},
        {"cat name", "NOVA"},
        {"my name", "SAMMY"},
        {"hes name", "LOSDRAGONS"}, -- will be overridden by best secret logic
        {"his name", "LOSDRAGONS"},
        {"best secret", "425M"},
        {"beast secret", "425M"},
        {"los dragons", "LOSDRAGONS"},
        {"my car", "RED"},
        {"my age", "24"},
    }
    for _, pair in ipairs(knownPhrases) do
        local phrase, val = pair[1], pair[2]
        local s = 1
        while true do
            local found = lower:find(phrase, s, true)
            if not found then break end
            table.insert(tokens, {pos=found, type="phrase", raw=phrase, value=val})
            s = found + #phrase
        end
    end
    
    -- Find standalone numbers that are NOT part of math
    -- We'll mark math ranges to avoid double counting
    local mathRanges = {}
    for _, t in ipairs(tokens) do
        if t.type == "math" then
            table.insert(mathRanges, {t.pos, t.pos + #t.raw -1})
        end
    end
    local function isInMathRange(pos)
        for _, r in ipairs(mathRanges) do
            if pos >= r[1] and pos <= r[2] then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    
    for start, num, finish in lower:gmatch("()(%d+)()") do
        if not isInMathRange(start) then
            -- Check if this number is part of a known phrase that already handled? We still want it
            -- But avoid if it's part of "with 67" -> we WANT it
            table.insert(tokens, {pos=start, type="number", raw=num, value=num})
        end
    end
    
    -- Sort by position
    table.sort(tokens, function(a,b) return a.pos < b.pos end)
    
    -- Deduplicate overlapping tokens (keep first)
    local final = {}
    local usedUntil = 0
    for _, t in ipairs(tokens) do
        if t.pos > usedUntil then
            table.insert(final, t)
            usedUntil = t.pos + #t.raw -1
            -- Special: for phrases, don't block numbers right after "with"
            if t.type == "phrase" then
                usedUntil = t.pos + #t.raw -1
                -- Allow numbers after to be included
                -- So reset a bit? Actually we want "my name with 67" -> my name + 67
                -- Our dedup would block 67 if it's within same range, but it's after, so ok
            end
        else
            -- If current is number and previous was phrase with "with", allow it
            if t.type == "number" then
                local prev = final[#final]
                if prev and prev.type == "phrase" then
                    -- check if between prev and this there's "with"
                    local between = lower:sub(prev.pos + #prev.raw, t.pos-1)
                    if between:find("with") then
                        table.insert(final, t)
                        usedUntil = t.pos + #t.raw -1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    return final
end

-- Override solveRiddle to use new token parser
local _oldSolveRiddleFix = solveRiddle
solveRiddle = function(question)
    if not question or question == "" then return nil end
    local lower = question:lower()
    
    -- Use new parser if contains numbers and keywords
    if lower:find("%d") and (lower:find("my name") or lower:find("cat name") or lower:find("and") or lower:find("with")) then
        local tokens = extractTokensInOrder(question)
        if #tokens > 1 then
            -- Build result preserving order, avoiding duplicate numbers that are part of math
            local parts = {}
            local seen = {}
            for _, t in ipairs(tokens) do
                if t.value and t.value ~= "" then
                    -- Avoid duplicate exact same value at same pos
                    local key = t.pos .. "_" .. t.value
                    if not seen[key] then
                        table.insert(parts, t.value)
                        seen[key] = true
                    end
                end
            end
            if #parts > 0 then
                -- Special logic: if question has "best secret" and "hes name", handle LOSDRAGONS
                if lower:find("best secret") or lower:find("beast secret") then
                    if lower:find("hes name") or lower:find("his name") or lower:find("what is") then
                        -- Replace SAMMY with LOSDRAGONS if best secret present
                        for i, v in ipairs(parts) do
                            if v == "SAMMY" then parts[i] = "LOSDRAGONS" end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return table.concat(parts, "")
            end
        end
    end
    
    -- Also handle simple "my name with 67" -> SAMMY67
    if lower:find("my name") and lower:find("with") then
        local num = lower:match("with%s+(%d+)")
        if num then
            return "SAMMY" .. num
        end
    end
    if lower:find("my cat name") and lower:find("with") then
        local num = lower:match("with%s+(%d+)")
        if num then
            return "NOVA" .. num
        end
    end
    
    return _oldSolveRiddleFix(question)
end

-- Test function for the specific case
getgenv().Test67 = function()
    print(solveRiddle("67+67 and my name with 67")) -- Should be 134SAMMY67
    print(solveRiddle("my name with 67")) -- Should be SAMMY67
    print(solveRiddle("67+67")) -- Should be 134
    print(solveRiddle("my name with 67 and 67+67")) -- Should be SAMMY67134
end

local function showGreenSpawnText()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    
    -- حذف القديم
    local old = playerGui:FindFirstChild("GreenSpawnText")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    
    local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    screenGui.Name = "GreenSpawnText"
    screenGui.Parent = playerGui
    screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(0.8,0,0.1,0)
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0.1,0,0.45,0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = "!Los dragons Spawned"
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) -- اخضر
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    label.TextScaled = true
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.Parent = screenGui
    
    -- صوت الصح
    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://12222025"
    sound.Volume = 1
    sound.Parent = label
    sound:Play()
    
    game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(screenGui, 3)
    print("[GREEN] !Los dragons Spawned")
end

-- ربط مع زر الجواب الصح
local function hookCorrectButton()
    spawn(function()
        while true do
            wait(0.3)
            local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
            local pg = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if pg then
                for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
                    if gui:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local txt = gui.Text:lower()
                        -- اذا زر الجواب
                        if txt:find("answer") or txt:find("submit") or txt:find("check") or txt:find("solve") or txt:find("confirm") then
                            if not gui:GetAttribute("GreenHooked") then
                                gui:SetAttribute("GreenHooked", true)
                                gui.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                                    wait(0.15)
                                    -- دور على علامة صح (اخضر)
                                    for _, lbl in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
                                        if lbl:IsA("TextLabel") then
                                            local ltxt = lbl.Text:lower()
                                            if (ltext and (ltext:find("correct") or ltext:find("صح") or ltext:find("✅"))) or lbl.TextColor3 == Color3.fromRGB(0,255,0) or lbl.TextColor3 == Color3.fromRGB(0,255,127) then
                                                showGreenSpawnText()
                                                break
                                            end
                                            -- اذا الجواب نفسه NOVA/SAMMY وصح
                                            if lbl.Text:find("Correct") or lbl.Text:find("صحيح") then
                                                showGreenSpawnText()
                                                break
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                    -- افتراضي اذا ضغط صح يطلع مباشرة
                                    showGreenSpawnText()
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- شغل الهوك
hookCorrectButton()

        showGreenSpawnText()
    end

        -- اذا تحققت الاجابة صحيحة
        if getgenv().lastAnswerCorrect then
            showGreenSpawnText()
        end
    end
    return result, ans
end

-- SPAWN NOTIFICATION + SOUND - V20
-- لما تجاوب رايدل صح يطلع Los dragons spawned! مع صوت
-- ============================================
local function createSpawnNotification(brainrotName)
    brainrotName = brainrotName or "Los dragons"
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    
    -- Remove old notification if exists
    local old = playerGui:FindFirstChild("SpawnNotif")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    
    -- Create ScreenGui
    local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    screenGui.Name = "SpawnNotif"
    screenGui.Parent = playerGui
    screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    
    -- Background frame (red like in image)
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0.15,0)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0.4,0)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = screenGui
    
    -- Text label (green with black outline like image)
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = brainrotName .. " spawned!"
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 255, 150)
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
    label.TextScaled = true
    label.Font = Enum.Font.FredokaOne -- bold rounded like game
    label.Parent = frame
    
    -- Animate
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,1,0)
    frame:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(0,0,0.4,0), Enum.EasingDirection.Out, Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce, 0.5, true)
    
    -- Auto remove after 3 seconds
    game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(screenGui, 3)
    
    -- Sound - صوت الصح لما تعمل ريدي كود
    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://12222025" -- صوت Correct / Success (تقدر تغيره)
    -- Alternative sounds: 12222030, 12222125, 12222058 (اصوات صح)
    -- For Los dragons special: 142376088 (spawn sound)
    sound.Volume = 1
    sound.Parent = frame
    sound:Play()
    
    -- Extra epic sound for Los dragons
    if brainrotName:lower():find("los dragons") or brainrotName:lower():find("dragon") then
        local epicSound = Instance.new("Sound")
        epicSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://12222216" -- صوت قوي
        epicSound.Volume = 0.8
        epicSound.Parent = frame
        epicSound:Play()
        game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(epicSound, 3)
    end
    
    print("[SPAWN] " .. brainrotName .. " spawned!")
end

-- Hook into testRiddleSolve - لما تضغط جواب ويطلع صح
local _oldTestRiddleSolve = testRiddleSolve
testRiddleSolve = function()
    local result, ans = _oldTestRiddleSolve()
    -- If answer is correct
    if result == true or (type(result) == "string" and result:find("صح") or result:find("Correct") or result:find("✅")) then
        -- Determine which brainrot was asked
        local question = ""
        if getgenv().lastRiddleQuestion then
            question = getgenv().lastRiddleQuestion
        end
        local lowerQ = question:lower()
        local spawnName = "Los dragons" -- default
        if lowerQ:find("best secret") or lowerQ:find("beast secret") or lowerQ:find("los dragons") then
            spawnName = "Los dragons"
        elseif lowerQ:find("my cat") then
            spawnName = "NOVA - My Cat"
        elseif lowerQ:find("griffin") then
            spawnName = "Griffin"
        elseif lowerQ:find("kraken") then
            spawnName = "Kraken"
        else
            spawnName = "Los dragons" -- أقوى سيكرت دايم
        end
        createSpawnNotification(spawnName)
    end
    return result, ans
end

                                        createSpawnNotification("Los dragons")

            end
        end
    end)
end

-- UTILITY & REDEEM LOGIC --
local function isOurGui(instance)
    local p = instance
    for _ = 1, 10 do
        if not p then break end
        if p.Name == "BroccoliHubUI" or p.Name == "SourcesHubRedeemerGui" or p.Name == "AAHelperUI" then return true end
        p = p.Parent
    end
    return false
end

local function isVisibleChain(inst)
    local current = inst
    while current do
        if current:IsA("GuiObject") and not current.Visible then return false end
        if current:IsA("ScreenGui") then return current.Enabled end
        current = current.Parent
    end
    return true
end

local function findAllTextBoxes(pg)
    local boxes = {}
    for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
        if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Enabled and not isOurGui(gui) then
            for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("TextBox") and not isOurGui(d) then
                    boxes[#boxes+1] = d
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return boxes
end

local function findCodeButtons(pg)
    local btns = {}
    for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
        if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Enabled and not isOurGui(gui) then
            for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) then
                    local n = d.Name:lower()
                    local pn = (d.Parent and d.Parent.Name or ""):lower()
                    if (n:find("code") or n:find("redeem") or pn:find("code") or pn:find("redeem")) and isVisibleChain(d) then
                        btns[#btns+1] = d
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return btns
end

local function clickButton(btn)
    if not btn then return false end
    local methods = {}
    methods[#methods+1] = function() btn.MouseButton1Click:Fire() end
    methods[#methods+1] = function() btn.Activated:Fire() end
    if typeof(firesignal) == "function" then
        methods[#methods+1] = function() firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Click) end
        methods[#methods+1] = function() firesignal(btn.Activated) end
    end
    if typeof(getconns) == "function" then
        methods[#methods+1] = function()
            local ok, cs = pcall(getconns, btn.MouseButton1Click)
            if ok and type(cs) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(cs) do pcall(function() c:Fire() end) end
            end
            local ok2, cs2 = pcall(getconns, btn.Activated)
            if ok2 and type(cs2) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(cs2) do pcall(function() c:Fire() end) end
            end
        end
    end
    if typeof(fireclick) == "function" then
        methods[#methods+1] = function() fireclick(btn) end
    end
    local anyOk = false
    for _, fn in ipairs(methods) do
        local ok = pcall(fn)
        anyOk = anyOk or ok
    end
    return anyOk
end

local function fireBoxFocusLost(box)
    if not box then return false end
    local anyFired = false
    if typeof(firesignal) == "function" then
        local ok = pcall(firesignal, box.FocusLost, true)
        anyFired = anyFired or ok
    end
    if typeof(getconns) == "function" then
        local ok, cs = pcall(getconns, box.FocusLost)
        if ok and type(cs) == "table" then
            for _, c in ipairs(cs) do
                local fn
                pcall(function() fn = c.Function end)
                if fn and typeof(getupvalues) == "function" and typeof(setupv) == "function" then
                    local uOk, ups = pcall(getupvalues, fn)
                    if uOk and type(ups) == "table" then
                        for i, v in pairs(ups) do
                            if type(v) == "boolean" and v == true then
                                pcall(setupv, fn, i, false)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                local fOk = pcall(function()
                    if c.Enabled ~= false then c:Fire(true) end
                end)
                anyFired = anyFired or fOk
            end
        end
    end
    return anyFired
end

local function typeAndSubmitCode(code)
    local pg = playerGui or player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if not pg then return false, "no PlayerGui" end

    local submitDelay = (_submitMode == "VIP") and 0.01 or 0.5

    local codesGui = pg:FindFirstChild("Codes")
    if codesGui then
        if codesGui:IsA("ScreenGui") then codesGui.Enabled = true end
        local codesFrame = codesGui:FindFirstChild("Codes") or codesGui
        if codesFrame then
            if codesFrame:IsA("GuiObject") then codesFrame.Visible = true end
            local cur = codesFrame
            while cur and cur ~= codesGui do
                if cur:IsA("GuiObject") then cur.Visible = true end
                cur = cur.Parent
            end
            local box = nil
            for _, d in ipairs(codesFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("TextBox") and not isOurGui(d) then box = d break end
            end
            local submitBtn = nil
            for _, d in ipairs(codesFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) then
                    local n = d.Name:lower()
                    local txt = ""
                    pcall(function() txt = d.Text:lower() end)
                    if n:find("submit") or txt:find("submit") or n:find("redeem") or txt:find("redeem") or n:find("claim") or n:find("confirm") or n:find("enter") then
                        submitBtn = d
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if not submitBtn then
                for _, d in ipairs(codesFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) then
                        local n = d.Name:lower()
                        if not n:find("close") and not n:find("x") and not n:find("toggle") then
                            submitBtn = d
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if box then
                pcall(function() box.Text = code end)
                task.wait(submitDelay)
                if submitBtn then clickButton(submitBtn) end
                fireBoxFocusLost(box)
                return true, "submitted via PlayerGui.Codes"
            end
        end
    end

    local btns = findCodeButtons(pg)
    for _, btn in ipairs(btns) do
        clickButton(btn)
        task.wait(submitDelay)
    end
    if _submitMode ~= "VIP" then task.wait(0.1) end

    local box = nil
    local deadline = tick() + 2
    while tick() < deadline do
        local allBoxes = findAllTextBoxes(pg)
        for _, d in ipairs(allBoxes) do
            if isVisibleChain(d) then
                local n = d.Name:lower()
                local pn = (d.Parent and d.Parent.Name or ""):lower()
                if n:find("code") or pn:find("code") or n:find("redeem") or pn:find("redeem") or n:find("input") or pn:find("textbox") or n:find("enter") then
                    box = d
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if not box then
            for _, d in ipairs(allBoxes) do
                if isVisibleChain(d) then box = d break end
            end
        end
        if box then break end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end

    if not box then return false, "no codebox visible" end
    pcall(function() box.Text = code end)
    task.wait(submitDelay)

    local redeemBtn = nil
    local searchNames = {"submit","redeem","claim","confirm","enter","send","apply","ok","use","go","check"}
    local p = box.Parent
    for _ = 1, 8 do
        if not p then break end
        for _, d in ipairs(p:GetDescendants()) do
            if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) and d ~= box then
                local n = d.Name:lower()
                local txt = ""
                pcall(function() txt = d.Text:lower() end)
                for _, sn in ipairs(searchNames) do
                    if n:find(sn) or txt:find(sn) then
                        if isVisibleChain(d) then redeemBtn = d break end
                    end
                end
                if redeemBtn then break end
            end
        end
        if redeemBtn then break end
        p = p.Parent
    end

    if redeemBtn then clickButton(redeemBtn) end
    fireBoxFocusLost(box)
    return true, "submitted via dynamic search"
end

local function aceCodeBox()
    local pg = playerGui
    local allBoxes = findAllTextBoxes(pg)
    for _, box in ipairs(allBoxes) do
        if isVisibleChain(box) then return box end
    end
    return nil
end

local COLORS = {
    Window = Color3.fromRGB(10, 8, 15),
    Row = Color3.fromRGB(20, 16, 28),
    Control = Color3.fromRGB(31, 24, 42),
    Log = Color3.fromRGB(8, 6, 12),
    Border = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
    White = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245),
    Text = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 220),
    Dim = Color3.fromRGB(175, 165, 190),
    Accent = Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 100),
    Green = Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 100),
    Red = Color3.fromRGB(218, 105, 105),
}

local function addCorner(parent, radius)
    local value = Instance.new("UICorner")
    value.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
    value.Parent = parent
    return value
end

local function addStroke(parent, color, thickness, transparency)
    local value = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    value.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    value.Color = color
    value.Thickness = thickness or 1
    value.Transparency = transparency or 0
    value.Parent = parent
    return value
end

local function makeLabel(parent, name, text, size, position, textSize, color, font)
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = name
    label.Size = size
    label.Position = position
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = text
    label.TextSize = textSize
    label.TextColor3 = color
    label.Font = font or Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.Parent = parent
    return label
end

local InterfaceScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
InterfaceScale.Name = "InterfaceScale"
InterfaceScale.Scale = 0.95
InterfaceScale.Parent = Window

local viewportConnection
local function updateInterfaceScale()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then InterfaceScale.Scale = 0.92; return end
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
    local fitScale = math.min((viewport.X - 20) / 340, (viewport.Y - 20) / 400)
    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
        local mobileTarget = 0.75
        InterfaceScale.Scale = math.max(0.48, math.min(mobileTarget, fitScale))
    else
        InterfaceScale.Scale = 0.92
    end
end

local function watchViewport()
    if viewportConnection then viewportConnection:Disconnect(); viewportConnection = nil end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then viewportConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateInterfaceScale) end
    updateInterfaceScale()
end
workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(watchViewport)
watchViewport()

local SnowContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
SnowContainer.Name = "SnowContainer"
SnowContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
SnowContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
SnowContainer.ClipsDescendants = true
SnowContainer.ZIndex = 2
SnowContainer.Parent = Window

local function createSnowflake()
    local flake = Instance.new("Frame")
    flake.Name = "Snowflake"
    flake.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    flake.BackgroundTransparency = math.random(15, 45) / 100
    flake.BorderSizePixel = 0
    flake.ZIndex = 2
    flake.Parent = SnowContainer

    local size = math.random(2, 5)
    flake.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
    addCorner(flake, size)

    local startX = math.random(0, 340)
    flake.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(startX, -10)

    local fallTime = math.random(35, 70) / 10
    local endX = startX + math.random(-40, 40)

    local tween = TweenService:Create(flake, TweenInfo.new(fallTime, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(endX, 410)
    })
    tween:Play()
    tween.Completed:Connect(function() flake:Destroy() end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while GUI and GUI.Parent do
        if math.random() > 0.35 then createSnowflake() end
        task.wait(0.12)
    end
end)

local Console, ConsoleOutput, updateConsoleCanvas
local featureStates = {}
local CONSOLE_COLORS = {
    Dim = "rgb(150,150,170)",
    Amber = "rgb(70,200,100)",
    Green = "rgb(70,200,100)",
    Red = "rgb(218,105,105)",
    Cyan = "rgb(200,200,255)",
    Luxury = "rgb(120,230,140)"
}

local function scrollConsoleToBottom()
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait()
        if not Console then return end
        if updateConsoleCanvas then updateConsoleCanvas() end
        local bottom = math.max(0, Console.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y - Console.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y)
        Console.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, bottom)
    end)
end

local function appendConsoleStatus(name, activated)
    if not ConsoleOutput then return end
    local state = type(activated) == "string" and activated or (activated and "ON" or "OFF")
    local stateColor = (activated == "VIP" or activated == true) and CONSOLE_COLORS.Green or (activated == "Normal" and CONSOLE_COLORS.Amber or CONSOLE_COLORS.Red)
    local line = '<font color="' .. CONSOLE_COLORS.Dim .. '">[setting]</font> ' .. '<font color="' .. CONSOLE_COLORS.Amber .. '">' .. name .. "</font> " .. '<font color="' .. CONSOLE_COLORS.Dim .. '">-&gt;</font> ' .. '<font color="' .. stateColor .. '">' .. tostring(state) .. "</font>"
    if ConsoleOutput.Text == "" then ConsoleOutput.Text = line else ConsoleOutput.Text = ConsoleOutput.Text .. "\n\n" .. line end
    scrollConsoleToBottom()
end

local lastToggleTime = 0
local function toggleAutoWrite()
    if tick() - lastToggleTime < 0.15 then return end
    lastToggleTime = tick()
    autoWriteEnabled = not autoWriteEnabled
    _enabled = autoWriteEnabled
    if not autoWriteEnabled and clearAceCapture then clearAceCapture() end
    savedConfig.codeSniper = autoWriteEnabled
    saveConfig()
    _lastStatusMsg = nil
    AutoWriteButton.BackgroundColor3 = autoWriteEnabled and COLORS.Accent or COLORS.Control
    AutoWriteStroke.Transparency = autoWriteEnabled and 0.25 or 0.6
    AutoWriteKnob.BackgroundColor3 = autoWriteEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 30) or Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 80)
    AutoWriteKnob.Position = autoWriteEnabled and UDim2.new(1, -22, 0.5, -10) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -10)
    if ConsoleOutput then
        if autoWriteEnabled then
            ConsoleOutput.Text = '<font color="' .. CONSOLE_COLORS.Amber .. '">&gt;</font> <font color="' .. CONSOLE_COLORS.Dim .. '">scanning for codes...</font>'
        else
            ConsoleOutput.Text = '<font color="' .. CONSOLE_COLORS.Dim .. '">status:</font> <font color="' .. CONSOLE_COLORS.Red .. '">Code Sniper OFF</font>\n<font color="' .. CONSOLE_COLORS.Dim .. '">Auto submit / Retype / Riddle still active</font>'
        end
        for _, featureName in ipairs({"Auto submit", "Submit mode", "Riddle solver", "Retype invalid"}) do
            if featureStates[featureName] ~= nil then appendConsoleStatus(featureName, featureStates[featureName]) end
        end
        scrollConsoleToBottom()
    end
end
AutoWriteButton.Activated:Connect(toggleAutoWrite)

local function makeStateButton(parent, enabled, consoleName, onToggle)
    parent.Active = true
    featureStates[consoleName] = enabled
    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Name = "State"
    button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 20)
    button.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, -10)
    button.BackgroundColor3 = enabled and COLORS.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 45)
    button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    button.AutoButtonColor = false
    button.Active = true
    button.Text = enabled and "ON" or "OFF"
    button.TextSize = 8
    button.TextColor3 = enabled and Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 30) or Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 190)
    button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    button.ZIndex = 6
    button.Parent = parent
    addCorner(button, 6)
    local outline = addStroke(button, COLORS.Border, 1, enabled and 0.25 or 0.55)
    local state = enabled
    local lastSubToggle = 0
    local function toggleState()
        if tick() - lastSubToggle < 0.15 then return end
        lastSubToggle = tick()
        state = not state
        featureStates[consoleName] = state
        button.Text = state and "ON" or "OFF"
        button.BackgroundColor3 = state and COLORS.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 45)
        button.TextColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 30) or Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 190)
        outline.Transparency = state and 0.25 or 0.55
        appendConsoleStatus(consoleName, state)
        if onToggle then onToggle(state) end
    end
    button.Activated:Connect(toggleState)
    return button
end

end

local RetypeCard = makeCard("RetypeInvalid", UDim2.fromOffset(17, 112), UDim2.fromOffset(135, 38))
makeLabel(RetypeCard, "Title", "★ Retype Pro", UDim2.new(1, -55, 1, 0), UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), 10, COLORS.Accent, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
