-- Window Dragging System --
do
    local dragging = false
    local activeDragInput
    local dragStart
    local startPosition
    local dragMoved = false
    local DRAG_THRESHOLD = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 10 or 3

    local function stopDragging(input)
        if input ~= activeDragInput then return end
        dragging = false
        activeDragInput = nil
        dragStart = nil
        startPosition = nil
    end

    local function isOverHeaderControl(position)
        local btnPos = AutoWriteButton.AbsolutePosition
        local btnSize = AutoWriteButton.AbsoluteSize
        return position.X >= (btnPos.X - 10) and position.X <= (btnPos.X + btnSize.X + 10) and position.Y >= (btnPos.Y - 10) and position.Y <= (btnPos.Y + btnSize.Y + 10)
    end

    Header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        if dragging or isOverHeaderControl(input.Position) then return end
        dragging = true
        activeDragInput = input
        dragStart = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        startPosition = Window.Position
        dragMoved = false
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End or input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then stopDragging(input) end
        end)
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging or not activeDragInput then return end
        local isTrackedTouch = activeDragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == activeDragInput
        local isTrackedMouse = activeDragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        if not isTrackedTouch and not isTrackedMouse then return end
        local current = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        local delta = current - dragStart
        if not dragMoved then
            if delta.Magnitude < DRAG_THRESHOLD then return end
            dragMoved = true
        end
        Window.Position = UDim2.new(startPosition.X.Scale, startPosition.X.Offset + delta.X, startPosition.Y.Scale, startPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end)
end

local function col3ToRich(col)
    if col == COLORS.Green then return CONSOLE_COLORS.Green end
    if col == COLORS.Red then return CONSOLE_COLORS.Red end
    if col == COLORS.Text then return CONSOLE_COLORS.Amber end
    if col == COLORS.White then return CONSOLE_COLORS.Cyan end
    if col == COLORS.Dim then return CONSOLE_COLORS.Dim end
    return string.format("rgb(%d,%d,%d)", math.floor(col.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(col.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(col.B * 255 + 0.5))
end

function setStatus(msg, col)
    if not ConsoleOutput then return end
    if msg == _lastStatusMsg then return end
    _lastStatusMsg = msg
    col = col or COLORS.Dim
    local line = '<font color="' .. col3ToRich(col) .. '">' .. tostring(msg) .. "</font>"
    if ConsoleOutput.Text == "" then ConsoleOutput.Text = line else ConsoleOutput.Text = ConsoleOutput.Text .. "\n\n" .. line end
    scrollConsoleToBottom()
end

function flashCode(code, col)
    if not code or code == "" or code == "—" then return end
    setStatus("[code] -> " .. tostring(code), col or COLORS.White)
end

local function resetPasteCounter()
    _capturedParts = {}
end

clearAceCapture = function()
    _capturedParts = {}
end

local function clearBoxWatchers()
    if _boxTextConn then pcall(function() _boxTextConn:Disconnect() end) end
    if _boxAncestryConn then pcall(function() _boxAncestryConn:Disconnect() end) end
    for _, connection in ipairs(_boxVisibilityConns) do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
    _boxTextConn = nil
    _boxAncestryConn = nil
    _boxVisibilityConns = {}
    _lastWatchedBox = nil
end

local function watchBoxForBlankReset(box)
    if not box or _lastWatchedBox == box then return end
    clearBoxWatchers()
    _lastWatchedBox = box
    if box.Text ~= "" then _lastNonBlankBoxText = box.Text end
    _boxTextConn = box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        if box.Text == "" then resetPasteCounter() else _lastNonBlankBoxText = box.Text end
    end)
    _boxAncestryConn = box.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
        if not parent then resetPasteCounter(); clearBoxWatchers() end
    end)
end

UserInputService.TextBoxFocused:Connect(function(box)
    if box:IsDescendantOf(GUI) then return end
    if box ~= aceCodeBox() then return end
    _focused = box
    _lastBox = box
    watchBoxForBlankReset(box)
    if _enabled then setStatus("Ready", COLORS.Green) end
end)

UserInputService.TextBoxFocusReleased:Connect(function(box)
    if box:IsDescendantOf(GUI) then return end
    local codeBox = aceCodeBox()
    if box ~= codeBox and box ~= _lastBox then return end
    if _retypeInvalid and rememberPendingSubmission and (box == codeBox or box == _lastBox) then
        local submittedText = box.Text ~= "" and box.Text or _lastNonBlankBoxText
        rememberPendingSubmission(box, submittedText, false)
    end
    if _focused == box then
        _focused = nil
        if _enabled then
            setStatus((_lastBox and _lastBox.Parent) and "Ready" or "Click code box first", (_lastBox and _lastBox.Parent) and COLORS.Green or COLORS.Dim)
        end
    end
end)

clearPendingSubmission = function()
    _pendingRejectedToken = _pendingRejectedToken + 1
    _pendingRejectedText = nil
    _pendingRejectedBox = nil
    _pendingRejectedUntil = 0
end

rememberPendingSubmission = function(box, text, replaceExisting)
    if not _retypeInvalid or not text or text == "" then return end
    if not replaceExisting and _pendingRejectedText and os.clock() <= _pendingRejectedUntil then return end
    _pendingRejectedToken = _pendingRejectedToken + 1
    local token = _pendingRejectedToken
    _pendingRejectedText = text
    _pendingRejectedBox = box
    _pendingRejectedUntil = os.clock() + 15
    task.delay(15, function()
        if token == _pendingRejectedToken then clearPendingSubmission() end
    end)
end

local function restoreRejectedText(box, previousText)
    if not _retypeInvalid or not previousText or previousText == "" then return false end
    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    local repasteBox = aceCodeBox() or box
    if not repasteBox or not isVisibleChain(repasteBox) then return false end
    local restored = pcall(function()
        repasteBox.Text = ""
        task.wait(0.05)
        repasteBox.Text = previousText
    end)
    if restored then
        _lastBox = repasteBox
        watchBoxForBlankReset(repasteBox)
    end
    return restored
end

handleRedemptionFeedback = function(text, feedbackObject)
    if not _retypeInvalid or not _pendingRejectedText then return end
    if os.clock() > _pendingRejectedUntil then clearPendingSubmission(); return end
    if feedbackObject and feedbackObject:IsDescendantOf(GUI) then return end
    local lower = tostring(text or ""):lower()
    local rejected = lower:find("invalid", 1, true) or lower:find("expired", 1, true) or lower:find("already", 1, true) or lower:find("doesn't exist", 1, true) or lower:find("not found", 1, true) or lower:find("rejected", 1, true) or lower:find("fail", 1, true)
    if not rejected then return end
    local previousText = _pendingRejectedText
    local previousBox = _pendingRejectedBox
    local restored = restoreRejectedText(previousBox, previousText)
    clearPendingSubmission()
    if restored then
        setStatus("✨ [Retype Pro] Anti-Loss Restored: " .. previousText, COLORS.Accent)
        flashCode(previousText, COLORS.Accent)
    end
end

function appendToBox(text)
    if not text or text == "" then return end
    if _lastWatchedBox and not isVisibleChain(_lastWatchedBox) then
        resetPasteCounter()
        clearBoxWatchers()
    end
    local box = aceCodeBox()
    table.insert(_capturedParts, text)
    local targetLimit = _submitAfter
    if #_capturedParts > targetLimit then table.remove(_capturedParts, 1) end
    local combinedCode = table.concat(_capturedParts, " ")
    local capturedCount = #_capturedParts

    if box then
        _lastBox = box
        watchBoxForBlankReset(box)
        local boxWasFocused = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == box
        box.Text = combinedCode
        if boxWasFocused then
            pcall(function()
                local caretEnd = #combinedCode + 1
                box.CursorPosition = caretEnd
                box.SelectionStart = caretEnd
            end)
        end
    else
        setStatus("Captured; opening & searching UI...", COLORS.Text)
    end

    setStatus("Pasted [" .. _submitMode .. "] " .. tostring(capturedCount) .. "/" .. tostring(targetLimit), COLORS.Green)
    flashCode(combinedCode, COLORS.Green)

    if capturedCount >= targetLimit then
        _capturedParts = {}
        if _autoAccept then
            rememberPendingSubmission(box, combinedCode, true)
            local ok, statusMsg = typeAndSubmitCode(combinedCode)
            if ok then
                setStatus("Redeemed (" .. _submitMode .. "): " .. combinedCode, COLORS.Green)
            else
                local restored = restoreRejectedText(box, combinedCode)
                clearPendingSubmission()
                if restored then
                    setStatus("✨ [Retype Pro] Invalid - Restored: " .. combinedCode, COLORS.Accent)
                    flashCode(combinedCode, COLORS.Red)
                else
                    setStatus("Failed: " .. tostring(statusMsg), COLORS.Red)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function watchRedemptionFeedbackObject(obj)
    if not (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton")) then return end
    handleRedemptionFeedback(obj.Text or "", obj)
    obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        handleRedemptionFeedback(obj.Text or "", obj)
    end)
end

for _, obj in ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants()) do
    watchRedemptionFeedbackObject(obj)
end
playerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
    task.wait(0.04)
    watchRedemptionFeedbackObject(obj)
end)

-- ANNOUNCEMENT NOTIFICATION LISTENER --
local function resolveNotifyRemote()
    if _G.PhiNotifyRemote then return _G.PhiNotifyRemote end
    local packages = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
    local Net = packages and packages:FindFirstChild("Net")
    if not Net then return nil end
    local getinfo = debug and debug.getinfo
    if getgc and getinfo and getconnections then
        for _, d in ipairs(Net:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                local ok, cs = pcall(getconnections, d.OnClientEvent)
                if ok then
                    for _, c in ipairs(cs) do
                        local f, fn = pcall(function() return c.Function end)
                        if f and type(fn) == "function" then
                            local i, info = pcall(getinfo, fn)
                            if i and info and tostring(info.short_src or info.source or ""):find("NotificationController", 1, true) then
                                return d
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function aceStripRich(text)
    if type(text) ~= "string" then return tostring(text) end
    return (text:gsub("<[^>]->", ""))
end

local function aceTokenize(text)
    local words = {}
    for word in text:gmatch("[%w_%d%p]+") do words[#words + 1] = word end
    return words
end

onAceAnnouncement = function(...)
    local text = aceStripRich(tostring((...) or ""))
    text = text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
    if text == "" then return end
    local words = aceTokenize(text)
    if #words == 0 then return end
    local captured = table.concat(words, " ")
    if _seen[captured] then return end
    _seen[captured] = true
    task.delay(1.25, function() _seen[captured] = nil end)
    appendToBox(captured)
end

local aceNotifyRemote = resolveNotifyRemote()
local aceListenConnection
if aceNotifyRemote then
    if getgenv then
        local previous = getgenv().BroccoliHubCodeNotifyConnection
        if previous then pcall(function() previous:Disconnect() end) end
    end
    aceListenConnection = aceNotifyRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
        if not _enabled then return end
        pcall(onAceAnnouncement, ...)
    end)
    if getgenv then getgenv().BroccoliHubCodeNotifyConnection = aceListenConnection end
end

if getgenv then
    getgenv().StopAura = function()
        if aceListenConnection then
            pcall(function() aceListenConnection:Disconnect() end)
            aceListenConnection = nil
        end
        if getgenv().BroccoliHubCodeNotifyConnection then
            pcall(function() getgenv().BroccoliHubCodeNotifyConnection:Disconnect() end)
            getgenv().BroccoliHubCodeNotifyConnection = nil
        end
        if GUI then GUI:Destroy() end
        pcall(function()
            local aa = game.CoreGui:FindFirstChild("AAHelperUI") or playerGui:FindFirstChild("AAHelperUI")
            if aa then aa:Destroy() end
        end)
    end
end

local AA_COLORS = {
    Window = Color3.fromRGB(10, 8, 15),
    Row = Color3.fromRGB(20, 16, 28),
    Control = Color3.fromRGB(31, 24, 42),
    Border = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
    White = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245),
    Accent = Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 100),
    Dim = Color3.fromRGB(175, 165, 190),
    Green = Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 130),
    Red = Color3.fromRGB(220, 100, 100),
}

local antiRagdollEnabled = false
local freezeEnabled = false

local originalWalkSpeed = 16
local originalJumpPower = 50
local originalJumpHeight = 7.2
local originalUseJumpPower = true
local freezeConnection = nil
local antiRagdollConnection = nil
local antiRagdollStateConnection = nil
local characterAddedConn = nil
local antiRagdollHumanoid = nil
local antiRagdollBackup = nil

local function getHumanoidAndRoot()
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return nil, nil end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return hum, root
end

local function saveOriginalMovement()
    local hum = select(1, getHumanoidAndRoot())
    if not hum then return end
    originalWalkSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed
    originalJumpPower = hum.JumpPower
    originalJumpHeight = hum.JumpHeight
    originalUseJumpPower = hum.UseJumpPower
end

local function applyFreeze(state)
    local hum, root = getHumanoidAndRoot()
    if not hum then return end
    if state then
        saveOriginalMovement()
        hum.WalkSpeed = 0
        hum.JumpPower = 0
        hum.JumpHeight = 0
        if root then root.Anchored = true end
    else
        hum.WalkSpeed = originalWalkSpeed
        hum.JumpPower = originalJumpPower
        hum.JumpHeight = originalJumpHeight
        hum.UseJumpPower = originalUseJumpPower
        if root then root.Anchored = false end
    end
end

local function backupAntiRagdollStates(hum)
    if antiRagdollBackup and antiRagdollBackup.Humanoid == hum then return end
    local function enabled(stateType)
        local ok, value = pcall(function() return hum:GetStateEnabled(stateType) end)
        return ok and value or true
    end
    antiRagdollBackup = {
        Humanoid = hum,
        FallingDown = enabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown),
        Ragdoll = enabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll),
        Physics = enabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics),
        PlatformStanding = enabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding),
        AutoRotate = hum.AutoRotate,
    }
end

local function applyAntiRagdoll(hum)
    if not hum or not hum.Parent then return end
    backupAntiRagdollStates(hum)
    pcall(function()
        hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
        hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
        hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false)
        hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding, false)
        hum.PlatformStand = false
        hum.AutoRotate = true
        local state = hum:GetState()
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        end
    end)
end

local function restoreAntiRagdoll()
    if not antiRagdollBackup then return end
    local hum = antiRagdollBackup.Humanoid
    if hum and hum.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, antiRagdollBackup.FallingDown)
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, antiRagdollBackup.Ragdoll)
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, antiRagdollBackup.Physics)
            hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding, antiRagdollBackup.PlatformStanding)
            hum.AutoRotate = antiRagdollBackup.AutoRotate
            hum.PlatformStand = false
        end)
    end
    antiRagdollBackup = nil
    antiRagdollHumanoid = nil
end

local function disconnectAntiRagdoll()
    if antiRagdollConnection then pcall(function() antiRagdollConnection:Disconnect() end); antiRagdollConnection = nil end
    if antiRagdollStateConnection then pcall(function() antiRagdollStateConnection:Disconnect() end); antiRagdollStateConnection = nil end
end

local function startAntiRagdoll()
    disconnectAntiRagdoll()
    local hum = select(1, getHumanoidAndRoot())
    if not hum then return end
    antiRagdollHumanoid = hum
    applyAntiRagdoll(hum)

    antiRagdollStateConnection = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
        if not antiRagdollEnabled or hum ~= antiRagdollHumanoid then return end
        if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
            or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
            task.defer(function()
                if antiRagdollEnabled and hum.Parent then
                    applyAntiRagdoll(hum)
                    pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp); hum.PlatformStand = false end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    antiRagdollConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not antiRagdollEnabled then return end
        local currentHum = select(1, getHumanoidAndRoot())
        if not currentHum then return end
        if currentHum ~= antiRagdollHumanoid then
            disconnectAntiRagdoll()
            antiRagdollHumanoid = currentHum
            antiRagdollBackup = nil
            applyAntiRagdoll(currentHum)
            antiRagdollStateConnection = currentHum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
                if not antiRagdollEnabled or currentHum ~= antiRagdollHumanoid then return end
                if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
                    or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                    or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                    or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
                    task.defer(function()
                        if antiRagdollEnabled and currentHum.Parent then
                            applyAntiRagdoll(currentHum)
                            pcall(function() currentHum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp); currentHum.PlatformStand = false end)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
        applyAntiRagdoll(currentHum)
    end)
end

local function stopAntiRagdoll()
    disconnectAntiRagdoll()
    restoreAntiRagdoll()
end

local function onCharacterAdded(char)
    task.wait(0.3)
    if freezeEnabled then applyFreeze(true) end
    if antiRagdollEnabled then startAntiRagdoll() end
end

if characterAddedConn then characterAddedConn:Disconnect() end
characterAddedConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(onCharacterAdded)
if player.Character then task.spawn(onCharacterAdded, player.Character) end

local AA_Scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
AA_Scale.Name = "AAScale"
AA_Scale.Scale = 1
AA_Scale.Parent = AA_Window

local function updateAAScale()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then AA_Scale.Scale = 1; return end
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
        local fit = math.min((viewport.X - 20) / 280, (viewport.Y - 20) / 172)
        AA_Scale.Scale = math.max(0.55, math.min(0.85, fit))
    else
        AA_Scale.Scale = 1
    end
end
workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(updateAAScale)
if workspace.CurrentCamera then workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateAAScale) end
updateAAScale()

-- ==================== FIXED RIDDLE TEST LOGIC ====================
local _riddleTestBusy = false
local _lastRiddleQuery = ""

local function doRiddleAATest()
    if _riddleTestBusy then return end
    _riddleTestBusy = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local q = (RiddleAA_Input.Text or ""):gsub("^%s+",""):gsub("%s+$","")
        if q == "" then
            RiddleAA_Status.Text = "Write question"
            RiddleAA_Status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,100,100)
            _riddleTestBusy = false
            return
        end

        RiddleAA_Status.Text = "Solving: "..q
        RiddleAA_Status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,200,100)

        local ok, ans = pcall(solveRiddle, q)
        if not ok then
            ans = nil
            RiddleAA_Status.Text = "Error: "..tostring(ans)
            RiddleAA_Status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,100,100)
            warn("[RiddleTest] solveRiddle error: "..tostring(ans))
        end

        if ans and ans ~= "" then
            _lastRiddleQuery = q
            RiddleAA_Status.Text = q.." -> "..ans
            RiddleAA_Status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100,255,100)
            if _lastBox then pcall(function() _lastBox.Text = ans end) end
            if setStatus then setStatus("[AA Riddle] "..q.." -> "..ans, COLORS.Accent) end
            if flashCode then flashCode(ans, COLORS.Accent) end
            if appendToBox then appendToBox(ans) end
        else
            local ok2, guesses = pcall(findClosestAnswers, q, 3)
            if ok2 and guesses and #guesses > 0 then
                RiddleAA_Status.Text = "Guesser: "..guesses[1]
                RiddleAA_Status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,200,0)
                if setStatus then setStatus("[AA Guesser] "..q.." -> "..guesses[1], COLORS.Accent) end
                if appendToBox then appendToBox(guesses[1]) end
            else
                RiddleAA_Status.Text = "No answer for: "..q
                RiddleAA_Status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,100,100)
            end
        end

        -- Release focus so next typing works cleanly
        pcall(function() RiddleAA_Input:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
        task.wait(0.1)
        _riddleTestBusy = false
    end)
end

-- Fix: use both MouseButton1Click (PC/Mobile reliable) and Activated
RiddleAA_Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(doRiddleAATest)
RiddleAA_Btn.Activated:Connect(doRiddleAATest)

-- Fix FocusLost: solve on Enter, and reset busy if needed
RiddleAA_Input.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
    if enterPressed then
        doRiddleAATest()
    end
end)

-- Fix: when user starts typing a new question, reset status to Ready
RiddleAA_Input:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
    local cur = (RiddleAA_Input.Text or ""):gsub("^%s+",""):gsub("%s+$","")
    if cur ~= "" and cur ~= _lastRiddleQuery then
        if RiddleAA_Status.Text ~= "Ready" and not RiddleAA_Status.Text:find("Solving") then
            -- keep current result until new typing, but if typing different, show Ready
            if cur:len() < _lastRiddleQuery:len() or not cur:find(_lastRiddleQuery,1,true) then
                RiddleAA_Status.Text = "Ready"
                RiddleAA_Status.TextColor3 = AA_COLORS.Dim
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- Fix initial text: use placeholder, not actual Text
RiddleAA_Input.Text = ""

-- Increase AA window height to fit new card
AA_Window.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(260, 260)


local function updateToggleVisual(btn, stroke, enabled)
    if enabled then
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = AA_COLORS.Accent
        btn.Text = "ON"
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 30)
        stroke.Transparency = 0.2
    else
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = AA_COLORS.Control
        btn.Text = "OFF"
        btn.TextColor3 = AA_COLORS.Dim
        stroke.Transparency = 0.5
    end
end

local lastARToggle = 0
AR_Toggle.Activated:Connect(function()
    if tick() - lastARToggle < 0.15 then return end
    lastARToggle = tick()
    antiRagdollEnabled = not antiRagdollEnabled
    updateToggleVisual(AR_Toggle, AR_Stroke, antiRagdollEnabled)
    if antiRagdollEnabled then startAntiRagdoll() else stopAntiRagdoll() end
end)

local lastFZToggle = 0
FZ_Toggle.Activated:Connect(function()
    if tick() - lastFZToggle < 0.15 then return end
    lastFZToggle = tick()
    freezeEnabled = not freezeEnabled
    updateToggleVisual(FZ_Toggle, FZ_Stroke, freezeEnabled)
    applyFreeze(freezeEnabled)
end)

do
    local dragging = false
    local activeDragInput
    local dragStart
    local startPosition
    local dragMoved = false
    local DRAG_THRESHOLD = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 10 or 3

    local function stopDragging(input)
        if input ~= activeDragInput then return end
        dragging = false
        activeDragInput = nil
        dragStart = nil
        startPosition = nil
    end

    AA_Header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        if dragging then return end
        dragging = true
        activeDragInput = input
        dragStart = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        startPosition = AA_Window.Position
        dragMoved = false
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End or input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then stopDragging(input) end
        end)
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging or not activeDragInput then return end
        local isTrackedTouch = activeDragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == activeDragInput
        local isTrackedMouse = activeDragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        if not isTrackedTouch and not isTrackedMouse then return end
        local current = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        local delta = current - dragStart
        if not dragMoved then
            if delta.Magnitude < DRAG_THRESHOLD then return end
            dragMoved = true
        end
        AA_Window.Position = UDim2.new(startPosition.X.Scale, startPosition.X.Offset + delta.X, startPosition.Y.Scale, startPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end)
end

if getgenv and getgenv().StopAura then
    local oldStop = getgenv().StopAura
    getgenv().StopAura = function()
        stopAntiRagdoll()
        if freezeEnabled then applyFreeze(false) end
        if characterAddedConn then characterAddedConn:Disconnect() end
        if AA_GUI then AA_GUI:Destroy() end
        oldStop()
    end
end


-- ============================================================
-- FINAL RIDDLE MATH + NUMBER FALLBACK - FARDI MODE v2
-- Fixes:
--   sammy , my cat name , 67 => SAMMYNOVA67 (فردي)
--   sammy and 67 => SAMMY67
--   my cat name and 67 => NOVA67
--   17+37 => 54
--   62+37 and 67 => 9967 (99 + 67)
--   62+37 , my name with 67 => 99SAMMY67
--   كل جزء بين فاصلة او and/with/&/، ينحل لحاله
-- ============================================================
do
    local _BROCCOLI_HUB_PreviousSolveRiddle = solveRiddle

    local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_trim(s)
        return tostring(s or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    end

    local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_solveMath(expr)
        if not expr then return nil end
        expr = _BROCCOLI_HUB_trim(expr):gsub("%s+", "")
        local a, op, b = expr:match("^(%d+)([%+%-])(%d+)$")
        if not a then
            a, op, b = expr:match("^(%d+)([%*/])(%d+)$")
        end
        a, b = tonumber(a), tonumber(b)
        if not a or not b or not op then return nil end
        if op == "+" then return tostring(a + b)
        elseif op == "-" then return tostring(a - b)
        elseif op == "*" then return tostring(a * b)
        elseif op == "/" then if b == 0 then return nil end return tostring(math.floor(a / b)) end
        return nil
    end

    local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_extractNumbersAndMath(question)
        local q = tostring(question or "")
        local items = {}
        local protected = {}
        for pos, expr in q:lower():gmatch("()(%d+%s*[%+%-*/]%s*%d+)") do
            local answer = _BROCCOLI_HUB_solveMath(expr)
            if answer then
                table.insert(items, { pos = pos, finish = pos + #expr - 1, value = answer, kind = "math" })
                table.insert(protected, { pos = pos, finish = pos + #expr - 1 })
            end
        end
        local function insideProtected(pos)
            for _, r in ipairs(protected) do
                if pos >= r.pos and pos <= r.finish then return true end
            end
            return false
        end
        for pos, num in q:gmatch("()(%d+)") do
            if not insideProtected(pos) then
                table.insert(items, { pos = pos, finish = pos + #num - 1, value = num, kind = "number" })
            end
        end
        table.sort(items, function(a, b)
            if a.pos == b.pos then return a.kind == "math" end
            return a.pos < b.pos
        end)
        return items
    end

    local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_fixTypos(s)
        s = tostring(s or "")
        local low = s:lower()
        -- اخطاء شائعة جدا
        low = low:gsub("^m%s+", "my "):gsub("^y%s+", "my "):gsub("^y%s*cat", "my cat"):gsub("^my%s*caat", "my cat")
        low = low:gsub("^m%s*cat", "my cat"):gsub("^my%s*ct", "my cat"):gsub("^m%s*car", "my car"):gsub("^my%s*cr", "my car")
        low = low:gsub("mycat", "my cat"):gsub("mycar", "my car"):gsub("myctname", "my cat name"):gsub("mcatname", "my cat name")
        low = low:gsub("my%s*cat%s*nmae", "my cat name"):gsub("my%s*cat%s*naem", "my cat name"):gsub("my%s*cat%s*nme", "my cat name")
        low = low:gsub("my%s*car%s*colr", "my car color"):gsub("my%s*car%s*coulr", "my car color")
        low = low:gsub("skin%s*colr", "skin color"):gsub("skin%s*colur", "skin color"):gsub("skn%s*color", "skin color"):gsub("sin%s*color", "skin color")
        low = low:gsub("coulr", "color"):gsub("colur", "color"):gsub("colr", "color"):gsub("collor", "color")
        low = low:gsub("samy", "sammy"):gsub("sami", "sammy"):gsub("spyder", "spider"):gsub("spydr", "spider"):gsub("spidr", "spider")
        low = low:gsub("٦٧", "67"):gsub("٦", "6"):gsub("٧", "7") -- ارقام عربية
        low = low:gsub("[^%w%s%+%-%*/]", " "):gsub("%s+", " "):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        return low
    end

    local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_guessAny(part)
        -- يخمن حتى لو السكور قليل جدا (لما يكون فيه غلط + رقم)
        part = _BROCCOLI_HUB_trim(part)
        if part == "" then return nil end
        local ok, scored = pcall(function()
            local q = part:lower()
            local qNoNums = q:gsub("%d+", " "):gsub("%s+", " "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
            local results = {}
            for k,v in pairs(RIDDLE_DB) do
                -- نحسب تشابه بسيط جدا
                local sim = 0
                if q:find(k,1,true) or k:find(q,1,true) then sim = 0.8 end
                -- لو فيه كلمة مشتركة
                for word in q:gmatch("%w+") do
                    if k:find(word,1,true) and #word>2 then sim = sim + 0.2 end
                end
                if sim > 0.1 then
                    table.insert(results, {key=k, ans=v, score=sim})
                end
            end
            table.sort(results, function(a,b) return a.score>b.score end)
            if #results>0 then return results[1].ans end
            return nil
        end)
        if ok and scored then return tostring(scored) end
        return nil
    end

    local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_resolveTextPart(part)
        part = _BROCCOLI_HUB_trim(part)
        if part == "" then return nil end
        local normalized = part:lower():gsub("سامي", "sammy"):gsub("سامی", "sammy")
        normalized = _BROCCOLI_HUB_fixTypos(normalized)
        
        -- 1. مباشر
        local ok, result = pcall(_BROCCOLI_HUB_PreviousSolveRiddle, normalized)
        if ok and result and tostring(result) ~= "" then return tostring(result) end
        local ok2, result2 = pcall(_BROCCOLI_HUB_PreviousSolveRiddle, part)
        if ok2 and result2 and tostring(result2) ~= "" then return tostring(result2) end
        local fixedOriginal = _BROCCOLI_HUB_fixTypos(part)
        local ok3, result3 = pcall(_BROCCOLI_HUB_PreviousSolveRiddle, fixedOriginal)
        if ok3 and result3 and tostring(result3) ~= "" then return tostring(result3) end
        
        -- 2. Guesser عادي
        local ok4, guesses = pcall(findClosestAnswers, normalized, 1)
        if ok4 and guesses and #guesses > 0 and guesses[1] and guesses[1] ~= "" then return tostring(guesses[1]) end
        local ok5, guesses2 = pcall(findClosestAnswers, part, 1)
        if ok5 and guesses2 and #guesses2 > 0 and guesses2[1] and guesses2[1] ~= "" then return tostring(guesses2[1]) end
        
        -- 3. Guesser متساهل جدا (اي غلط + رقم)
        local anyGuess = _BROCCOLI_HUB_guessAny(normalized)
        if anyGuess then return anyGuess end
        local anyGuess2 = _BROCCOLI_HUB_guessAny(part)
        if anyGuess2 then return anyGuess2 end
        
        return nil
    end

    local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_splitFardi(q)
        -- نحول كل الفواصل لكلمة | ثم نقسم
        local s = tostring(q or "")
        s = s:gsub("،", ",") -- عربي
        -- نحول و  العربية ل and
        s = s:gsub("%s+و%s+", " and ")
        -- نوحد الفواصل
        s = s:gsub("%s*,%s*", "|")
        s = s:gsub("%s*;%s*", "|")
        s = s:gsub("%s+and%s+", "|")
        s = s:gsub("%s+&%s+", "|")
        s = s:gsub("%s+with%s+", "|")
        s = s:gsub("%s+plus%s+", "|")
        s = s:gsub("%s+\+%s+", "|")
        -- ننظف || متكررة
        s = s:gsub("||+", "|")
        local parts = {}
        for p in s:gmatch("[^|]+") do
            p = _BROCCOLI_HUB_trim(p)
            if p ~= "" then table.insert(parts, p) end
        end
        return parts
    end

    -- ============================================================
    -- SMART DATE / DAY / MONTH / YEAR SOLVER (dynamic realtime)
    -- ============================================================
    local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_getNow()
        local t = os.date("*t")
        local dayNames = {"SUNDAY","MONDAY","TUESDAY","WEDNESDAY","THURSDAY","FRIDAY","SATURDAY"}
        local monthNames = {"JANUARY","FEBRUARY","MARCH","APRIL","MAY","JUNE","JULY","AUGUST","SEPTEMBER","OCTOBER","NOVEMBER","DECEMBER"}
        local dayName = dayNames[t.wday] or "SATURDAY"
        local monthName = monthNames[t.month] or "SEPTEMBER"
        local dayNum = tostring(t.day)
        local year = tostring(t.year)
        local monthNum = string.format("%02d", t.month)
        local dayNumPad = string.format("%02d", t.day)
        return {
            dayName = dayName,
            monthName = monthName,
            dayNum = dayNum,
            year = year,
            monthNum = monthNum,
            dayNumPad = dayNumPad,
            fullDate = monthName .. dayNum .. year,          -- SEPTEMBER262026
            dateShort = monthName .. dayNum,                 -- SEPTEMBER26
            iso = year .. monthNum .. dayNumPad,             -- 20260926
            slash = monthNum .. "/" .. dayNumPad .. "/" .. year,
            dayMonth = dayName .. monthName,                 -- SATURDAYSEPTEMBER
            monthYear = monthName .. year,                   -- SEPTEMBER2026
            dayYear = dayName .. year,                       -- SATURDAY2026
        }
    end

    local function _BROCCOLI_HUB_solveSmartDate(question)
        if not question or question == "" then return nil end
        local q = tostring(question):lower()
        -- normalize common typos / informal
        q = q:gsub("[%?%!%.%,%:%;]+", " ")
        q = q:gsub("%s+", " "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
        q = q:gsub("todays", "today")
        q = q:gsub("today'?s", "today")
        q = q:gsub("wut", "what"):gsub("wat", "what"):gsub("wht", "what")
        q = q:gsub("goin+", "going"):gsub("gona", "going"):gsub("gonna", "going")
        q = q:gsub("currnt", "current"):gsub("curent", "current"):gsub("curerent", "current")
        q = q:gsub("monthe?", "month"):gsub("yeare?", "year"):gsub("daay", "day"):gsub("dte", "date")
        q = q:gsub("teh", "the"):gsub("th e", "the")

        -- skip if clearly about birth / sab / game history (static DB handles those)
        if q:find("birth", 1, true) or q:find("born", 1, true) or q:find("created", 1, true)
            or q:find("released", 1, true) or q:find("made", 1, true) or q:find("sab", 1, true)
            or q:find("game", 1, true) or q:find("sammy age", 1, true) or q:find("how old", 1, true) then
            return nil
        end

        local now = _BROCCOLI_HUB_getNow()

        -- DAY OF THE MONTH (number) — must be BEFORE day-of-week
        if q:find("day of the month", 1, true) or q:find("day of month", 1, true)
            or q:match("day%s+of%s+the%s+month") or q:match("day%s+of%s+month")
            or q:match("the%s+day%s+of%s+the%s+month") or q:match("what%s+day%s+of%s+the%s+month")
            or q:match("which%s+day%s+of%s+the%s+month") or q:match("todays?%s+day%s+of%s+the%s+month")
            or q:match("day%s+number") or q:match("date%s+number") or q:match("the%s+day%s+number")
            or q:match("what%s+day%s+number") or q:match("number%s+of%s+the%s+day")
            or q:match("what%s+is%s+the%s+day%s+of%s+the%s+month") then
            return now.dayNum
        end

        -- DAY OF WEEK
        if q:match("day%s+of%s+the%s+week") or q:match("day%s+of%s+week")
            or q:match("^what%s+day") or q:match("^which%s+day") or q:match("^day%s+today")
            or q:match("^today%s+day") or q:match("^current%s+day")
            or q:match("day%s+is%s+it") or q:match("day%s+going") or q:match("day%s+now")
            or q:match("what%s+day%s+is") or q:match("whats?%s+the%s+day")
            or q:match("^day$") or q:match("^the%s+day$")
            or q:match("what%s+day%s+are%s+we") or q:match("what%s+day%s+we")
            or q:match("todays?%s+day") or q:match("day%s+it%s+is")
            or (q:match("^the%s+day") and not q:find("month", 1, true)) then
            return now.dayName
        end

        -- MONTH
        if q:match("^what%s+month") or q:match("^which%s+month") or q:match("^month%s+today")
            or q:match("^today%s+month") or q:match("^current%s+month") or q:match("^this%s+month")
            or q:match("month%s+is%s+it") or q:match("month%s+now") or q:match("month%s+going")
            or q:match("whats?%s+the%s+month") or q:match("^month$") or q:match("^the%s+month$")
            or q:match("todays?%s+month") or q:match("what%s+month%s+are%s+we") then
            return now.monthName
        end

        -- YEAR
        if q:match("^what%s+year") or q:match("^which%s+year") or q:match("^year%s+today")
            or q:match("^today%s+year") or q:match("^current%s+year") or q:match("^this%s+year")
            or q:match("year%s+is%s+it") or q:match("year%s+now") or q:match("year%s+going")
            or q:match("whats?%s+the%s+year") or q:match("^year$") or q:match("^the%s+year$")
            or q:match("todays?%s+year") or q:match("what%s+year%s+are%s+we")
            or q:match("what%s+year%s+is%s+it") then
            return now.year
        end

        -- DATE
        if q:match("^what%s+date") or q:match("^which%s+date") or q:match("^date%s+today")
            or q:match("^today%s+date") or q:match("^current%s+date") or q:match("^todays?%s+date")
            or q:match("date%s+is%s+it") or q:match("date%s+now") or q:match("whats?%s+the%s+date")
            or q:match("^date$") or q:match("^the%s+date$") or q:match("what%s+is%s+the%s+date")
            or q:match("full%s+date") or q:match("complete%s+date") then
            return now.fullDate
        end

        -- day + month
        if q:match("day%s+and%s+month") or q:match("month%s+and%s+day") or q:match("day%s+%&%s+month")
            or q:match("day%s+with%s+month") or q:match("month%s+with%s+day") then
            return now.dayName .. now.monthName
        end

        -- month + year
        if q:match("month%s+and%s+year") or q:match("year%s+and%s+month") or q:match("month%s+with%s+year") then
            return now.monthName .. now.year
        end

        -- day + year
        if q:match("day%s+and%s+year") or q:match("year%s+and%s+day") or q:match("day%s+with%s+year") then
            return now.dayName .. now.year
        end

        -- day + month + year / full
        if q:match("day%s+month%s+year") or q:match("full%s+date") or q:match("complete%s+date")
            or q:match("date%s+full") or q:match("today%s+full") then
            return now.fullDate
        end

        -- short date month+day
        if q:match("month%s+day") or q:match("date%s+short") or q:match("short%s+date") then
            return now.dateShort
        end

        -- very short / loose matches (fallback layer)
        if q:find("day", 1, true) and (q:find("today", 1, true) or q:find("now", 1, true) or q:find("current", 1, true) or q:find("going", 1, true) or q:find("what", 1, true) or q:find("which", 1, true)) then
            if not q:find("month", 1, true) and not q:find("year", 1, true) and not q:find("date", 1, true) and not q:find("number", 1, true) then
                return now.dayName
            end
        end
        if q:find("month", 1, true) and (q:find("today", 1, true) or q:find("now", 1, true) or q:find("current", 1, true) or q:find("this", 1, true) or q:find("what", 1, true) or q:find("which", 1, true)) then
            if not q:find("year", 1, true) and not q:find("day", 1, true) then
                return now.monthName
            end
        end
        if q:find("year", 1, true) and (q:find("today", 1, true) or q:find("now", 1, true) or q:find("current", 1, true) or q:find("this", 1, true) or q:find("what", 1, true) or q:find("which", 1, true)) then
            if not q:find("month", 1, true) and not q:find("day", 1, true) and not q:find("birth", 1, true) then
                return now.year
            end
        end
        if q:find("date", 1, true) and (q:find("today", 1, true) or q:find("now", 1, true) or q:find("current", 1, true) or q:find("what", 1, true) or q:find("which", 1, true)) then
            return now.fullDate
        end

        return nil
    end

    -- expose for testing
    getgenv().TestSmartDate = function()
        local tests = {
            "what day", "what day is it", "what day today", "what day going",
            "day today", "current day", "today day", "which day", "day of week",
            "the day of the month today", "day of the month", "day of month",
            "what day of the month", "day number", "the day number",
            "what month", "current month", "this month", "month today",
            "what year", "current year", "this year", "year now",
            "what date", "today date", "current date", "the date",
            "day and month", "month and year", "day and year",
            "full date", "what day number",
        }
        for _, tq in ipairs(tests) do
            local ans = _BROCCOLI_HUB_solveSmartDate(tq)
            print("[SMART DATE]", tq, "=>", tostring(ans))
        end
        local n = _BROCCOLI_HUB_getNow()
        print("[NOW]", n.dayName, n.monthName, n.dayNum, n.year, n.fullDate)
    end

    -- Hook into text part resolver (highest priority)
    local _oldResolve = _BROCCOLI_HUB_resolveTextPart
    _BROCCOLI_HUB_resolveTextPart = function(part)
        local dateAns = _BROCCOLI_HUB_solveSmartDate(part)
        if dateAns then return dateAns end
        return _oldResolve(part)
    end

    solveRiddle = function(question)
        if question == nil then return nil end
        local original = tostring(question)
        local q = _BROCCOLI_HUB_trim(original)
        if q == "" then return nil end

        -- SMART DATE first (highest priority for pure date questions)
        local smartDate = _BROCCOLI_HUB_solveSmartDate(q)
        if smartDate then return smartDate end

        -- محاولة الحل المباشر بدون ارقام اولا
        local hasNumber = q:find("%d") ~= nil
        local hasMathOp = q:match("%d+%s*[%+%-*/]%s*%d+")
        local exactOK, exactAns = pcall(_BROCCOLI_HUB_PreviousSolveRiddle, q)
        
        -- لو مفيش ارقام، رجع الحل المباشر على طول
        if not hasNumber and exactOK and exactAns and tostring(exactAns) ~= "" then
            return exactAns
        end

        -- لو فيه رقم، لازم نحل فردي (FARDI MODE)
        -- نقسم الجملة لاجزاء
        local fardiParts = _BROCCOLI_HUB_splitFardi(q)
        
        -- لو جزء واحد بس ومفيش فاصلة، استخدم الطريقة القديمة للتوافق
        if #fardiParts <= 1 and not hasMathOp then
            -- لو فيه and/with في النص الاصلي بس فشل التقسيم، جرب تقسيم اكثر تفصيلا
            if q:lower():find(" and ") or q:lower():find(" with ") then
                fardiParts = _BROCCOLI_HUB_splitFardi(q) -- اعادة
            end
        end

        local finalResult = {}

        for _, rawPart in ipairs(fardiParts) do
            local part = _BROCCOLI_HUB_trim(rawPart)
            if part ~= "" then
                local tokens = _BROCCOLI_HUB_extractNumbersAndMath(part)
                if #tokens == 0 then
                    -- نص صافي
                    local ans = _BROCCOLI_HUB_resolveTextPart(part)
                    if ans then
                        table.insert(finalResult, ans)
                    end
                else
                    -- فيه ارقام/رياضة جوة الجزء ده، نحل اللي قبله وبعده فردي
                    local cursor = 1
                    for _, token in ipairs(tokens) do
                        local before = _BROCCOLI_HUB_trim(part:sub(cursor, token.pos - 1))
                        before = before:gsub("^[%s,;:]+", ""):gsub("[%s,;:]+$", "")
                        if before ~= "" and before:lower() ~= "and" and before:lower() ~= "with" then
                            local ans = _BROCCOLI_HUB_resolveTextPart(before)
                            if ans then table.insert(finalResult, ans) end
                        end
                        table.insert(finalResult, token.value)
                        cursor = token.finish + 1
                    end
                    local after = _BROCCOLI_HUB_trim(part:sub(cursor))
                    after = after:gsub("^[%s,;:]+", ""):gsub("[%s,;:]+$", "")
                    if after ~= "" and after:lower() ~= "and" and after:lower() ~= "with" then
                        local ans = _BROCCOLI_HUB_resolveTextPart(after)
                        if ans then table.insert(finalResult, ans) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if #finalResult > 0 then
            local finalAnswer = table.concat(finalResult, "")
            -- لو النتيجة رقم واحد بس وكان فيه حل ادق من الـ DB، استخدم الـ DB لو مفيش فاصلة
            if #finalResult == 1 and #fardiParts == 1 and exactOK and exactAns and tostring(exactAns) ~= finalResult[1] then
                -- لو السؤال كله رقم واحد بس، فضل الـ DB
                if not hasMathOp and q:lower():find(" and ") == nil and q:lower():find(" with ") == nil and q:find(",") == nil then
                    return exactAns
                end
            end
            return finalAnswer
        end

        if exactOK then return exactAns end
        return nil
    end

    getgenv().TestRiddleMath = function()
        local tests = {
            "sammy",
            "sammy, 67",
            "sammy and 67",
            "sammy , my cat name , 67",
            "my cat name and 67",
            "my cat name, my car color and 67",
            "17+37",
            "62+37",
            "62+37 and 67",
            "62+37 and 67 and 8",
            "my name with 67",
            "67 and my name with 67",
            "17+37 and my name with 67",
            "sammy, 17+37 and my cat name",
        }
        for _, qtest in ipairs(tests) do
            local ok, ans = pcall(solveRiddle, qtest)
            print("[RIDDLE TEST]", qtest, "=>", ok and tostring(ans) or "ERROR: "..tostring(ans))
        end
    end
end
-- ============================================================
-- END FINAL RIDDLE MATH + NUMBER FALLBACK - FARDI v2
-- ============================================================
